实现泰拉瑞亚 1.4.5.6 复刻仓库 game/（cwd）的 **Gore 死亡碎块系统全量化**。标杆=../Terarria1456/ 反编译源码，所有数值/行为引行号。素材已全量拷入 public/sprites/vanilla/Gore_*.png（1403 张）。

背景：仓库已有零散 gore 使用（Tombstone/Boss 专属/落叶族 Gore_910-925 等在 wind/落叶系统）。本任务做**通用 gore 管线**：任意 NPC 死亡→按原版表炸出对应 gore 碎块。

步骤：
1. **数据提取**：写 tools/extract-gore.mjs——从 Terraria/NPC.cs 的 HitEffect（`private void HitEffect`，约 :81813-83100+，含 hitEffect/deathEffect 双段：life<=0 段为死亡 gore）逐 type 提取 `Gore.NewGore(position, velocity, goreId, scale)` 调用表 → src/data/vanilla-gore.json（{npcId: [{id, vx?, vy?, scale?}]}；velocity 常见 (Main.rand.NextVector2Circular?)近似记录数量与速度系数）。**只提取带具体数字 id 的调用**；运行时计算的 id（如 `num + Next()`）保留表达式近似（记录原始表达式字符串）。跑通提取器落盘。
2. **实体**：新建 src/entities/GorePiece.ts——字段 pos/v/rot/vr/life/timeLeft/sticky（落地吸附）/goreId；物理 1:1 参照 Gore.cs（../Terarria1456/Terraria/Gore.cs）：重力 0.3?（查源码 Update：velocity.Y+=0.3 落地 sticky 后 rotation 归零、alpha 渐隐 timeLeft）；帧=贴图帧（gore 贴图多帧竖条，帧数=高/帧高，帧高查 Gore.cs/常量——Gore 每张贴图 framesFromSheet? 用 vanilla 惯例 1 列 N 行循环，间隔查源码）。绘制由 Renderer 钩子做（见 4）。
3. **死亡钩**：src/entities/Enemy.ts（你独占）——死亡路径（onEnemyKilled 的上游、hurt 尾部 kill 处；找到 checkDead/kill 等价点）查 vanilla-gore.json：有表项则 spawn 各 GorePiece（速度=原版 Next 范围近似：随机 ±3），Boss 用 def.boss 时也照表。玩家击杀/任意死亡都出。**先 Read Enemy.ts 再 Edit**（该文件被并行会话频繁改动）。
4. **渲染**：src/render/Renderer.ts 新增 private drawGorePieces(ctx, cam)（帧动画+alpha 渐隐+落地 sticky），在实体绘制段附近加一行分发。⚠Renderer.ts 有其他并行代理同时小改：**每次 Edit 前先 Read 最新内容**，锚点用稳定注释行，只做加法。
5. **测试**：tests/gore.test.ts——提取表健全性（覆盖≥300 npcId）、GorePiece 物理（重力/落地 sticky/渐隐）、僵尸/史莱姆死亡出 gore（mock GameHooks）。
验收：node tools/extract-gore.mjs 落表；vitest 你的测试绿；tsc 你的文件 0 错。最后汇报：表覆盖数/物理参数/接入点行号/遗留（表达式中无法静态求值的 id 列表）。

244		private void UpdateLightningBunnySparks()
245		{
246			if (frameCounter == 0)
247			{
248				frameCounter = 1;
249				Frame.CurrentRow = (byte)Main.rand.Next(3);
250			}
251			timeLeft -= GoreID.Sets.DisappearSpeed[type];
252			if (timeLeft <= 0)
253			{
254				active = false;
255				return;
256			}
257			alpha = (int)MathHelper.Lerp(255f, 0f, (float)timeLeft / 15f);
258			float num = (255f - (float)alpha) / 255f;
259			num *= scale;
260			Lighting.AddLight(position + new Vector2(Width / 2f, Height / 2f), num * 0.4f, num, num);
261			position += velocity;
262		}

264		private float ChumFloatingChunk_GetWaterLine(int X, int Y)
265		{
266			float result = position.Y + Height;
267			if (Main.tile[X, Y - 1] == null)
268			{
269				Main.tile[X, Y - 1] = new Tile();
270			}
271			if (Main.tile[X, Y] == null)
272			{
273				Main.tile[X, Y] = new Tile();
274			}
275			if (Main.tile[X, Y + 1] == null)
276			{
277				Main.tile[X, Y + 1] = new Tile();
278			}
279			if (Main.tile[X, Y - 1].liquid > 0)
280			{
281				result = Y * 16;
282				result -= (float)(Main.tile[X, Y - 1].liquid / 16);
283			}
284			else if (Main.tile[X, Y].liquid > 0)
285			{
286				result = (Y + 1) * 16;
287				result -= (float)(Main.tile[X, Y].liquid / 16);
288			}
289			else if (Main.tile[X, Y + 1].liquid > 0)
290			{
291				result = (Y + 2) * 16;
292				result -= (float)(Main.tile[X, Y + 1].liquid / 16);
293			}
294			return result;
295		}

297		private bool DeactivateIfOutsideOfWorld()
298		{
299			Point point = position.ToTileCoordinates();
300			if (!WorldGen.InWorld(point.X, point.Y))
301			{
302				active = false;
303				return true;
304			}
305			if (Main.tile[point.X, point.Y] == null)
306			{
307				active = false;
308				return true;
309			}
310			return false;
311		}

18	  if (!tombstoneCanvas) {
19	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
20	    c.width = 16; c.height = 22;
21	    const x = c.getContext('2d')!;
22	    const stone = '#A8A8B4', dark = '#70707E', light = '#C4C4D0', en = '#585864';
23	    x.fillStyle = stone;
24	    x.fillRect(4, 2, 8, 2);
25	    x.fillRect(3, 4, 10, 10);
26	    x.fillRect(2, 14, 12, 3);
27	    x.fillStyle = dark;
28	    x.fillRect(1, 17, 14, 2);
29	    x.fillRect(2, 19, 12, 1);
30	    x.fillStyle = light;
31	    x.fillRect(3, 5, 1, 11);
32	    x.fillStyle = dark;
33	    x.fillRect(12, 5, 1, 11);
34	    x.fillStyle = en;
35	    x.fillRect(6, 6, 4, 1);
36	    x.fillRect(5, 9, 6, 1);
37	    x.fillRect(6, 12, 4, 1);
38	    tombstoneCanvas = c;
39	  }
40	  return tombstoneCanvas;
41	}

43	/** DropTombstone 款式选择 → 原版 style（0-10） */
44	function pickStyle(coinsOwned: number): number {
45	  const roll = Math.floor(Math.random() * 6); // Main.rand.Next(6)
46	  let type: number;
47	  if (coinsOwned <= 100000) {
48	    type = roll !== 0 ? 200 + roll : 43;
49	  } else {
50	    type = 527 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
51	  }
52	  // Projectile.cs:23696-23700 的 type→style 换算
53	  if (type === 43) return 0;
54	  if (type >= 201 && type <= 205) return type - 200;
55	  return type - 527 + 6; // 527-531 → 6-10
56	}
57	
58	/** 原版长日期（en: "MMMM d, yyy"；zh culture: ToLongDate） */

60	  return new Date().toLocaleDateString('zh-CN', { year: 'numeric', month: 'long', day: 'numeric' });
61	}

63	export class Tombstone extends Entity {
64	  w = 14; h = 14;

72	
73	  constructor(x: number, y: number, coinsOwned: number, deathText: string, hitDirection: number) {
74	    super();
75	    this.style = pickStyle(coinsOwned);
76	    this.epitaph = `${deathText}\n${longDate()}`;
77	    // DropTombstone L38446-38449：随机游走修正直到 |num|≥2（范围 ±3.5）
78	    let num = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 71) - 35) * 0.1;
79	    while (num < 2 && num > -2) num += (Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) - 30) * 0.1;
80	    this.x = x - this.w / 2;
81	    this.y = y - this.h / 2;
82	    this.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 21) + 10) * 0.1 * hitDirection + num;
83	    this.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 21) - 40) * 0.1; // -2 .. -4 上抛
84	  }
85	
86	  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
87	    // aiStyle17（Projectile.cs:23677-23686）：落地摩擦 → 翻滚 → 重力
88	    if (this.vy === 0) this.vx *= 0.98;
89	    this.angle += this.vx * 0.1;
90	    // aiStyle17 专用重力 0.2/帧（Projectile.cs:23681，非全局 GRAVITY 0.36）
91	    this.vy = Math.min(this.vy + 0.2, MAX_FALL_SPEED);
92	    moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);
93	    // 落格检查（L23689-23717）：底缘中心下方格可放墓碑 → 放置 + 碑文 + 消失
94	    const bx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / 16);
95	    const by = Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 4) / 16); // 脚下贴地的行
96	    const top = by - 1;                                 // 2×2 物件顶行（底行与脚对齐）
97	    if (this.tryPlace(game, bx, top)) {
98	      this.dead = true;
99	      return;
100	    }
101	    void dt;
102	  }
103	
104	  /** TileObject.CanPlace(85) 近似：2×2 全空 + 底下两格实心支撑 */
105	  private tryPlace(game: GameHooks, x: number, y: number): boolean {
106	    const st = game.world.store;
107	    const tid = TILE_BY_KEY['tombstone_v'];
108	    if (tid === undefined) return false;
109	    if (x < 1 || y < 1 || x + 1 >= st.w || y + 2 >= st.h) return false;
110	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
111	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
112	        if (st.isActive(x + dx, y + dy)) return false;
113	      }
114	    }
115	    if (!st.isSolid(x, y + 2) || !st.isSolid(x + 1, y + 2)) return false;
116	    // 放置（TileObject.Place 帧语义：frameX = style*36 + dx*18，frameY = dy*18）
117	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
118	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
119	        st.setTile(x + dx, y + dy, tid, this.style * 36 + dx * 18, dy * 18);
120	      }
121	    }
122	    this.placedAt = { x, y };
123	    game.world.signs.push({ x, y, text: this.epitaph });
124	    game.playSfx('dig');
125	    return true;
126	  }
127	
128	  draw() { /* Renderer 统一绘制 */ }
129	}
130

39	  dead: boolean;
40	}
41	
42	/** 墓地云雾（Gore 1087-1093，SpecialAI 4=地面云 / 5=空中云链） */
43	interface Cloud {
44	  x: number; y: number;
45	  vx: number; vy: number;
46	  /** Gore.alpha：0=不透明 → 255=全透明（GetAlpha :1486 opacity=(255-alpha)/255） */
47	  alpha: number;
48	  timeLeft: number;
49	  scale: number;
50	  type: number;
51	  rotation: number;
52	  airborne: boolean;
53	  dead: boolean;
54	}
55	
56	/** 盒碰撞（Gore 走 Collision.TileCollision 3-14px 盒）：逐轴探测，撞轴归零并回报 */
57	function boxCollide(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, size: number): { nx: number; ny: number; nvx: number; nvy: number; hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
58	  let nx = x, ny = y, hitX = false, hitY = false;
59	  if (vx !== 0) {
60	    const edge = vx > 0 ? x + size + vx : x + vx;
61	    const tx = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
62	    let blocked = false;
63	    for (let ty = Math.floor((y + 1) / TILE); ty <= Math.floor((y + size - 1) / TILE); ty++) {
64	      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }
65	    }
66	    if (blocked) { hitX = true; } else nx = x + vx;
67	  }
68	  if (vy !== 0) {
69	    const edge = vy > 0 ? y + size + vy : y + vy;
70	    const ty = Math.floor(edge / TILE);
71	    let blocked = false;
72	    for (let tx = Math.floor((x + 1) / TILE); tx <= Math.floor((x + size - 1) / TILE); tx++) {
73	      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) { blocked = true; break; }
74	    }
75	    if (blocked) { hitY = true; } else ny = y + vy;
76	  }
77	  return { nx, ny, nvx: hitX ? 0 : vx, nvy: hitY ? 0 : vy, hitX, hitY };
78	}
79	
80	export class NatureParticles {
81	  leaves: Leaf[] = [];
82	  drips: Drip[] = [];
83	  clouds: Cloud[] = [];
84	  private st: TileStore | null = null;
85	  maxLeaves = 220;
86	  maxDrips = 90;
87	  /** 云雾池上限（原版共用 600 格 gore 池；云雾独占上限防挤占视效预算） */
88	  maxClouds = 160;
89	  /** 滴水溅落音钩子（Game 注入 Sfx；Gore.cs :971-984：SoundID 39=Drip，
90	   *  落地 variant rand(0,1) / 入水 variant 2，位置 +8,+8；flag4 水型本模块内静音） */
91	  onDripSplash: ((x: number, y: number, wet: boolean) => void) | null = null;
92	
93	  clear(): void { this.leaves.length = 0; this.drips.length = 0; this.clouds.length = 0; this.windCounter = 0; this.airSpots.length = 0; }
94	  /** Game 每帧绑定当前世界 store */
95	  bindStore(st: TileStore): void { this.st = st; }
96	
97	  private liquidAt(px: number, py: number): number {
98	    const st = this.st;
99	    if (!st) return 0;
100	    const tx = Math.floor(px / TILE), ty = Math.floor(py / TILE);
101	    if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) return 0;
102	    const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
103	    return st.liquid[i] > 0 ? st.liquidType[i] : 0; // 1水 2岩浆 3蜂蜜 4微光
104	  }
105	
106	  // ================= 落叶（Gore_UpdateLeaf :1104） =================
107	
108	  spawnLeaf(x: number, y: number, gore: number): void {
109	    if (this.leaves.length >= this.maxLeaves || !LEAF_GORES.has(gore)) return;
110	    const row = Math.floor(Math.random() * 8);
111	    this.leaves.push({
112	      x, y,
113	      vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, vy: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, // NewGore 初速 :1423
114	      row, rowGroup: Math.floor(row / 4), rowCounter: Math.floor(Math.random() * 8), // :1425-1428
115	      alpha: 0, timeLeft: 900,
116	      scale: 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6, // :8012
117	      gore, dead: false,
118	    });
119	  }
120	
121	  /** 每固定 tick（Game.fixedUpdate）。wind = Main.WindForVisuals；worldSurfacePx=地表线像素 */
122	  updateLeaves(wind: number, worldSurfacePx: number): void {
123	    const st = this.st;
124	    if (!st) return;
125	    for (const l of this.leaves) {
126	      if (l.dead) continue;
127	      if (l.vy < 0) {
128	        // ---- 落地/滑移支（:1109-1150）----
129	        let moveX = l.vx;
130	        const liq = this.liquidAt(l.x + 4, l.y + 2);
131	        if (liq > 0) {
132	          // 蜂蜜 X=0 / 岩浆消亡 / 水 X=风力；地下 X=0（:1128-1140）
133	          moveX = liq === 3 ? 0 : wind;
134	          if (l.y > worldSurfacePx) moveX = 0;
135	        } else {
136	          const tx = Math.floor((l.x + 4) / TILE), ty = Math.floor((l.y + 4) / TILE);
137	          if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty + 1)) {
138	            l.vy = 0.1; l.timeLeft = 0; l.alpha = Math.min(255, l.alpha + 20); // :1136-1140
139	          }
140	        }
141	        const r = boxCollide(st, l.x, l.y, moveX, -0.2, 3); // vector3=(vx,-0.2) 盒 3px（:1115-1118）
142	        l.x = r.nx; l.y = r.ny;
143	        l.vx = r.nvx * 0.94; // :1145
144	        if (Math.abs(l.vx) < 0.01) l.vx = 0; // |Δ|<0.01 → 0（:1146）
145	        if (l.timeLeft > 0) l.timeLeft -= 1; // DisappearSpeed=1
146	        else l.alpha = Math.min(255, l.alpha + 1); // DisappearSpeedAlpha=1
147	        if (l.alpha >= 255) l.dead = true;
148	        continue;
149	      }
150	      // ---- 下落支（:1151-1215）----
151	      l.vy += Math.PI / 180; // :1151
152	      let mx = -Math.sin(l.vy);
153	      let my = Math.abs(Math.cos(l.vy)); // UnitY.RotatedBy(vy)，Y 取 |·|（:1152）
154	      if (l.y < worldSurfacePx) mx += wind * 4; // :1155-1157
155	      const r = boxCollide(st, l.x + 4, l.y, mx, my, 4);
156	      l.x = r.nx - 4; l.y = r.ny;
157	      if (r.hitX || r.hitY) l.vy = -1; // 碰撞 → 落地支（:1187）
158	      else {
159	        // 液面浮叶（:1207+ 与落地支同语义）：水面受风/地下静止
160	        const liq = this.liquidAt(l.x + 6, l.y + 6);
161	        if (liq > 0 && liq !== 2) {
162	          l.vy = -1;
163	          l.vx = l.y > worldSurfacePx ? 0 : wind;
164	        } else if (liq === 2) {
165	          l.dead = true; // 岩浆烧叶（:1130-1134）
166	        }
167	      }
168	      // 帧推进（Gore.Update :687-697：8 tick 一换，行在 4 帧组内循环）
169	      if (++l.rowCounter >= 8 && l.vy > 0.2) {
170	        l.rowCounter = 0;
171	        l.row++;
172	        if (l.row >= 4 + l.rowGroup * 4) l.row = l.rowGroup * 4;
173	      }
174	    }
175	    this.leaves = this.leaves.filter((l) => !l.dead);
176	  }
177	
178	  // ================= 滴水（Gore.cs :383-530 + 碰撞 :955-985） =================
179	
180	  spawnDrip(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
181	    if (this.drips.length >= this.maxDrips || !DRIP_GORES.has(type)) return;
182	    this.drips.push({ x, y, vy: 0, type, frame: 0, frameCounter: 0, alpha: 0, dead: false });
183	  }
184	
185	  private spawnChildFall(d: Drip): void {
186	    // frame==5 时生成下落子滴（:446-448：NewGore frame=9、速度清零）
187	    if (this.drips.length >= this.maxDrips) return;
188	    this.drips.push({ x: d.x, y: d.y, vy: 0, type: d.type, frame: 9, frameCounter: 0, alpha: 0, dead: false });
189	  }
190	
191	  updateDrips(worldSurfacePx: number): void {
192	    const st = this.st;
193	    if (!st) return;
194	    for (const d of this.drips) {
195	      if (d.dead) continue;
196	      // alpha（:385-398）：943/1160-62 恒 0；地表以下 100；地表 0
197	      if (d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) d.alpha = 0;
198	      else if (d.y < worldSurfacePx + 8) d.alpha = 0;
199	      else d.alpha = 100;
200	      d.frameCounter++;
201	      let wait = 4;
202	      if (d.frame <= 4) {
203	        // 蓄滴（:399-449）：上方非活性格消亡；各帧随机等待
204	        const tx = Math.floor(d.x / TILE), ty = Math.floor(d.y / TILE) - 1;
205	        if (st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)]) { d.dead = true; continue; }
206	        if (d.frame <= 2) wait = 24 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
207	        else if (d.frame === 3) wait = 24 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 96);
208	        // 原版另有 frame==5 特例(16+Next(64))——在 frame<=4 块内不可达=死代码，不移植
209	        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
210	        if (d.type === 717) wait *= 4;
211	        if ((d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) && d.frame < 6) wait = 4;
212	        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) {
213	          d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++;
214	          if (d.frame === 5) this.spawnChildFall(d);
215	        }
216	      } else if (d.frame <= 6) {
217	        wait = 8;
218	        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
219	        if (d.type === 717) wait *= 3;
220	        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) {
221	          d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++;
222	          if (d.frame === 7) { d.dead = true; continue; }
223	        }
224	      } else if (d.frame <= 9) {
225	        // 下落（:472-508）：6 tick 一帧，重力按水型；clamp [0.5,12]
226	        wait = 6;
227	        if (d.type === 716) { wait = Math.floor(wait * 1.5); d.vy += 0.175; }
228	        else if (d.type === 717) { wait *= 2; d.vy += 0.15; }
229	        else if (d.type === 943) { wait = Math.floor(wait * 1.5); d.vy += 0.2; }
230	        else d.vy += 0.2;
231	        if (d.vy < 0.5) d.vy = 0.5;
232	        if (d.vy > 12) d.vy = 12;
233	        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) { d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++; if (d.frame > 9) d.frame = 7; }
234	        const preVy = d.vy;
235	        const r = boxCollide(st, d.x, d.y, 0, d.vy, 14); // TileCollision(position, velocity, 16, 14)
236	        d.x = r.nx; d.y = r.ny; d.vy = r.nvy;
237	        if (r.nvy !== preVy) {
238	          if (d.frame < 10) { d.frame = 10; d.frameCounter = 0; this.dripSound(d, false); } // :968
239	          continue;
240	        }
241	        const liq = this.liquidAt(d.x + 8, d.y + 8);
242	        if (liq > 0 && d.frame < 10) { d.frame = 10; d.frameCounter = 0; this.dripSound(d, true); } // WetCollision :980
243	      } else {
244	        // 溅落 10-14（:510-529）：速度清零、逐帧消散
245	        wait = 4;
246	        if (d.type === 716) wait *= 2;
247	        else if (d.type === 717) wait *= 6;
248	        d.vy += 0.1;
249	        if (d.frameCounter >= wait) { d.frameCounter = 0; d.frame++; }
250	        d.vy = 0;
251	        if (d.frame > 14) d.dead = true;
252	      }
253	    }
254	    this.drips = this.drips.filter((d) => !d.dead);
255	  }
256	
257	  /** 溅落音（Gore.cs :967-984）：flag4 水型（716 蜂蜜/717/943/1160-62）不发声 */
258	  private dripSound(d: Drip, wet: boolean): void {
259	    if (d.type === 716 || d.type === 717 || d.type === 943 || (d.type >= 1160 && d.type <= 1162)) return;
260	    this.onDripSplash?.(d.x + 8, d.y + 8, wet);
261	  }
262	
263	  // ================= 墓地环境云雾（AmbientWindSystem.cs + Gore SpecialAI 4/5） =================
264	  // AmbientWindSystem.Update（:23-52）：ZoneGraveyard 才产云；工作区 = 玩家中心
265	  // 120×30 格；逐格 TrySpawningWind（地面云 1/120 + 1/3 复生上层、空中云位
266	  // 1/120000 候选），每 30 tick 消化空中云位 → SpawnAirborneCloud 链。
267	
268	  private windCounter = 0;
269	  private airSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
270	
271	  /** AmbientWindSystem.Update + 既有云雾推进。出墓地只停生成，已生云雾照常漂散
272	   *  （原版 gore 池独立于 Zone 判定） */
273	  updateAmbientWind(graveyard: boolean, wind: number, px: number, py: number): void {
274	    const st = this.st;
275	    if (!st) return;
276	    for (const c of this.clouds) {
277	      if (c.dead) continue;
278	      if (c.airborne) this.stepAirborneCloud(c);
279	      else this.stepFloorCloud(c, wind);
280	    }
281	    if (this.clouds.length > 0) this.clouds = this.clouds.filter((c) => !c.dead);
282	    if (!graveyard) { this.windCounter = 0; this.airSpots.length = 0; return; }
283	    this.windCounter++;
284	    const cx = Math.floor(px / TILE), cy = Math.floor(py / TILE);
285	    const x0 = cx - 60, y0 = cy - 15;                       // GetTileWorkSpace（:60-66）
286	    for (let x = x0; x < x0 + 120; x++) {
287	      for (let y = y0; y < y0 + 30; y++) {
288	        this.trySpawningWind(x, y);
289	      }
290	    }
291	    if (this.windCounter % 30 === 0) {                      // :46-49
292	      for (const s of this.airSpots) this.spawnAirborneCloud(s.x, s.y, wind);
293	      this.airSpots.length = 0;
294	    }
295	  }
296	
297	  /** AmbientWindSystem.TrySpawningWind（:69-93） */
298	  private trySpawningWind(x: number, y: number): void {
299	    const st = this.st!;
300	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || x < 10 || y < 10 || x >= st.w - 10 || y >= st.h - 10) return; // InWorld(,10)
301	    this.testAirCloud(x, y);
302	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
303	    if (st.type[i] === 0 || st.slope[i] > 0 || st.half[i] || !st.isSolid(x, y)) return;
304	    if (!st.isSolid(x, y - 1) && Math.random() < 1 / 120) {
305	      this.spawnFloorCloud(x, y);
306	      if (Math.random() < 1 / 3) this.spawnFloorCloud(x, y - 1);   // :89-92
307	    }
308	  }
309	
310	  /** TestAirCloud（:151-166）：1/120000 且十字 ±2 格全允许风 → 空中云位 */
311	  private testAirCloud(x: number, y: number): void {
312	    if (Math.random() >= 1 / 120000) return;
313	    const st = this.st!;
314	    for (let d = -2; d <= 2; d++) {
315	      if (d === 0) continue;
316	      if (!this.tileAllowsWind(x + d, y) || !this.tileAllowsWind(x, y + d)) return;
317	    }
318	    this.airSpots.push({ x, y });
319	  }
320	
321	  /** DoesTileAllowWind（:168-172）：空气/非实心格允许风 */
322	  private tileAllowsWind(x: number, y: number): boolean {
323	    const st = this.st!;
324	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
325	    return st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === 0 || !st.isSolid(x, y);
326	  }
327	
328	  /** SpawnFloorCloud（:121-133）：(x,y-1) 格中心出生，上浮 0-16px；
329	   *  上浮 <4px 换贴地皮型 1090，否则 1087-1089；vx = 0.4×视觉风 */
330	  private spawnFloorCloud(x: number, y: number): void {
331	    if (this.clouds.length >= this.maxClouds) return;
332	    const lift = 16 * Math.random();
333	    const type = lift < 4 ? 1090 : 1087 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
334	    this.clouds.push({
335	      x: x * TILE + 8, y: (y - 1) * TILE + 8 - lift,
336	      vx: 0, vy: 0, alpha: 0, timeLeft: 600,          // NewGore 初始（Gore.cs:1347-1352）
337	      scale: 0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2, type, rotation: 0, airborne: false, dead: false,
338	    });
339	  }
340	
341	  /** SpawnAirborneCloud（:80-118）：沿缓转向航向排 2-5（1/4 时 9-15）朵云链，
342	   *  贴型 1091/1093 交替，尺度 1.1+rand×2.2（变体 ×1.2/×0.2） */
343	  private spawnAirborneCloud(x: number, y: number, wind: number): void {
344	    let n = 2 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 4);        // Next(2,6)
345	    let baseScale = 1.1, varScale = 2.2;
346	    if (Math.random() < 0.25) { n = 9 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7); baseScale = 1.1; varScale = 1.2; }
347	    else if (Math.random() < 0.25) { n = 9 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7); baseScale = 1.1; varScale = 0.2; }
348	    const TURN = 0.023561945;
349	    let turn = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);
350	    let step = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);
351	    while (step > -0.011780973 && step < 0.011780973) {
352	      step = TURN * (Math.random() * 2 - 1);
353	    }
354	    turn -= step * n * 0.5;                            // :89
355	    let px = x * TILE + 8, py = y * TILE + 8;
356	    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
357	      if (Math.random() < 0.1) step *= Math.random() * 2 - 1;   // :94-97
358	      const jx = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 4, jy = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 4;  // NextVector2Circular(4,4)
359	      const type = 1091 + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0 : 2);        // 1091 + Next(2)*2
360	      const scale = baseScale + Math.random() * varScale;
361	      const ang = turn + step;
362	      const dx = Math.cos(ang) * 1.4, dy = Math.sin(ang) * 1.4; // UnitX.RotatedBy(num9)*1.4
363	      if (this.clouds.length < this.maxClouds) {
364	        this.clouds.push({
365	          x: px + jx - 10, y: py + jy,                // vector2 + vector3 - (10,0)
366	          vx: dx * wind, vy: dy * wind,               // vector4 * WindForVisuals
367	          alpha: 0, timeLeft: 600, scale, type, rotation: ang, airborne: true, dead: false,
368	        });
369	      }
370	      px += dx * 6.5 * scale;                          // vector2 += vector4 * 6.5 * num8
371	      turn = ang;
372	    }
373	  }
374	
375	  /** UpdateAmbientFloorCloud（Gore.cs:111-148）：贴地漂移；头部格实心（钻墙）或
376	   *  下方两格全空（悬空）或 timeLeft≤30 → 淡出至 255 消散 */
377	  private stepFloorCloud(c: Cloud, wind: number): void {
378	    c.timeLeft -= 1;                                   // DisappearSpeed 默认 1
379	    if (c.timeLeft <= 0) { c.dead = true; return; }
380	    const st = this.st!;
381	    const hx = Math.floor((c.x + 15) / TILE), hy = Math.floor(c.y / TILE);
382	    if (!st.inBounds(hx, hy) || hy + 2 >= st.h) { c.dead = true; return; }
383	    let flag = st.isSolid(hx, hy) || (!st.isSolid(hx, hy + 1) && !st.isSolid(hx, hy + 2));
384	    if (c.timeLeft <= 30) flag = true;
385	    c.vx = 0.4 * wind;
386	    if (!flag) { if (c.alpha > 220) c.alpha--; }
387	    else {
388	      c.alpha++;
389	      if (c.alpha >= 255) { c.dead = true; return; }
390	    }
391	    c.x += c.vx;
392	  }
393	
394	  /** UpdateAmbientAirborneCloud（Gore.cs:157-202）：航向 = 速度角；头部格实心或
395	   *  timeLeft≤60 → 1/5 概率逐帧淡出 */
396	  private stepAirborneCloud(c: Cloud): void {
397	    c.timeLeft -= 1;
398	    if (c.timeLeft <= 0) { c.dead = true; return; }
399	    const st = this.st!;
400	    const hx = Math.floor((c.x + 15) / TILE), hy = Math.floor(c.y / TILE);
401	    if (!st.inBounds(hx, hy)) { c.dead = true; return; }
402	    c.rotation = Math.atan2(c.vy, c.vx);
403	    let flag = st.isSolid(hx, hy);
404	    if (c.timeLeft <= 60) flag = true;
405	    if (!flag) { if (c.alpha > 240 && Math.random() < 0.2) c.alpha--; }
406	    else {
407	      if (Math.random() < 0.2) c.alpha++;
408	      if (c.alpha >= 255) { c.dead = true; return; }
409	    }
410	    c.x += c.vx;
411	    c.y += c.vy;
412	  }
413	
414	  // ================= 绘制 =================
415	
416	  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas): void {
417	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
418	    // 落叶：32 列(油漆) × 8 行(旋转)，帧 12×14（SpriteFrame(32,8)）
419	    for (const l of this.leaves) {
420	      const img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${l.gore}.png`);
421	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
422	      const fw = img.naturalWidth / 32, fh = img.naturalHeight / 8;
423	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - l.alpha / 255;
424	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, l.row * fh, fw, fh, l.x, l.y, fw * l.scale, fh * l.scale);
425	    }
426	    // 滴水：18×300 竖条 15 帧（帧高 20）
427	    for (const d of this.drips) {
428	      const img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${d.type}.png`);
429	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
430	      const fh = img.naturalHeight / 15;
431	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - d.alpha / 255;
432	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, d.frame * fh, img.naturalWidth, fh, d.x, d.y, img.naturalWidth, fh);
433	    }
434	    // 云雾：Main.DrawGore（Main.cs:21749-21756）——中心锚 + scale + rotation，
435	    // 不透明度 = (255-alpha)/255（Gore.GetAlpha :1486）
436	    for (const c of this.clouds) {
437	      const img = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${c.type}.png`);
438	      if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) continue;
439	      const w = img.naturalWidth, h = img.naturalHeight;
440	      ctx.save();
441	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, (255 - c.alpha) / 255);
442	      ctx.translate(c.x + w / 2, c.y + h / 2);
443	      if (c.rotation) ctx.rotate(c.rotation);
444	      if (c.scale !== 1) ctx.scale(c.scale, c.scale);
445	      ctx.drawImage(img, -w / 2, -h / 2);
446	      ctx.restore();
447	    }
448	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
449	  }

454	/** EmitLiquidDrops 的水型映射（TileDrawing :7749-7785）。
455	 *  waterStyle：12→1147、13/14→706、>1→706+style-1、其余 706；
456	 *  374→716、375→717、461→943(腐化 1160/猩红 1161/神圣 1162)、709→1383 */
457	export function dripGoreFor(sheet: number, waterStyle: number, zone: { corrupt?: boolean; crimson?: boolean; hallow?: boolean }): number {
458	  if (sheet === 374) return 716;
459	  if (sheet === 375) return 717;
460	  if (sheet === 461) {
461	    if (zone.crimson) return 1161;
462	    if (zone.hallow) return 1162;
463	    if (zone.corrupt) return 1160;
464	    return 943;
465	  }
466	  if (sheet === 709) return 1383;
467	  if (waterStyle === 12) return 1147;
468	  if (waterStyle === 13 || waterStyle === 14) return 706;
469	  if (waterStyle > 1) return 706 + waterStyle - 1;
470	  return 706;
471	}
472	
473	/** EmitTreeLeaves 的喷出排除表（:7919-7940：-1/912/913/1278 不出叶） */
474	export function leafEmits(gore: number): boolean {
475	  return gore !== -1 && gore !== 912 && gore !== 913 && gore !== 1278;
476	}
477	
478	/** GetTreeLeaf（WorldGen.cs:29540-29756 1:1）：树型/草型 → 落叶 gore id。
479	 *  markerX = 冠/枝标记格 X；grassSheet = 树下草地格的原版 sheet */
480	export function getTreeLeaf(markerX: number, frameX: number, frameY: number, treeSheet: number, grassSheet: number): number {
481	  let treeFrame = 0;
482	  if (frameX === 22 || frameX === 44 || frameX === 66) {
483	    if (frameY === 220) treeFrame = 1;
484	    else if (frameY === 242) treeFrame = 2;
485	  }
486	  switch (treeSheet) {
487	    case 596: return 1248;
488	    case 616: return 1257;
489	    case 634: return 1278;
490	    case 583: case 584: case 585: case 586: case 587: case 588: case 589:
491	      return 1249 + (treeSheet - 583);
492	  }
493	  switch (grassSheet) {
494	    case 2: case 477: return 910;
495	    case 60: return 914;
496	    case 70: return 912;
497	    case 23: case 112: return 915;
498	    case 199: case 234: return 916;
499	    case 53: return 911;
500	    case 116: return 919;
501	    case 147: return 913;
502	    case 633: return 1278;
503	    case 109: case 492: {
504	      // 中空树：x%3 错帧 + treeFrame 0-8 映射（:29632-29663）→ +917 = 917-925
505	      if (markerX % 3 === 1) treeFrame += 3;
506	      if (markerX % 3 === 2) treeFrame += 6;
507	      const MAP = [2, 1, 7, 4, 5, 6, 3, 8, 0];
508	      return 917 + (MAP[treeFrame] ?? 2);
509	    }
510	    default:
511	      return -1; // 原版 switch 无 default 分支——未知草型 passStyle=-1，不出叶。
512	      // 注：109/492 的 style20 子支（→1113-1121 彩叶）依赖 GetHollowTreeFoliageStyle
513	      // 种子派生值，取 style!=20 支（917-925）为准——中空树为稀有种子景观。
514	  }
515	}

36	  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {

50	      return;
51	    }

53	    this.y += this.vy;

// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
import { TILE, GRAVITY } from '../core/constants';
import type { World } from '../world/World';

export interface Body {
  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
  w: number; h: number;
  vx: number; vy: number;
  onGround: boolean;
  hitWall: boolean;
  hitHead: boolean;
  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
  dropThrough?: boolean;
  /** 启用原版自动上台阶（Collision.StepUp）：行走实体（玩家/敌人/小动物/城镇NPC）置 true；
   *  掉落物/弹幕/墓碑等不参与（原版它们不调 StepUp） */
  stepUp?: boolean;
  /** StepDown 触发门。原版两处均为 == 精确等值（NPC.cs:54374 velocity.Y==0、
   *  Player.cs:23252 velocity.Y==gravity）——语义 = 仅"贴地行走"吸附,空中实体不吸附。
   *  本仓 NPC 族重力在碰撞【前】累加（贴地时 vy=GRAVITY≠0）,故 NPC 门取语义等价的
   *  onGround；玩家取原版字面 vy===GRAVITY（'gravity'）。
   *  ★曾用 vy>=0 宽门：低空飞行小动物（萤火虫/蝴蝶平飞 vy≥0）脚下 7~17px 有落面
   *  即被瞬移按压 → "怪力按地+闪现"周期循环（飞行 AI 抬升后再次触发） */
  stepDownGate?: 'grounded' | 'gravity';
  /** 液体位移减速因子（原版 waterMovementSpeed 族，NPC.cs:5946：水/岩浆 .5、蜂蜜 .25、
   *  微光 .375；dry=1）。Entity 基类缺省 1，Enemy 侧由 npcLiquid.updateNpcLiquid 写入 */
  liqFactor?: number;
}

/** Collision.StepUp（Collision.cs:3641-3770，gravDir=1、holdsMatching=false）：
 *  水平行走【意图速度】先探前方一列 → 满足净空门即抬升最多 16.1px
 *  （半砖 8px / 整砖台阶 16px）。玩家（Player.cs:23258/:27753）与全部 NPC
 *  （NPC.cs:54382，先于 TileCollision 用未受阻的速度调用）共用——
 *  此前只有玩家有自研版上台阶，敌人/小动物/城镇 NPC 全部卡死在半砖/台阶。
 *  gfxOffY 视觉缓动无对应渲染通道，位置直接抬升（原版 NPC 同样瞬间抬）。 */
function applyStepUp(b: Body, world: World): void {
  const vx = b.vx;
  if (vx === 0) return;
  const dir = Math.sign(vx);
  const st = world.store;
  const probeX = b.x + vx;                       // vector.X = position.X + velocity.X
  const num2 = Math.floor((probeX + b.w / 2 + (b.w / 2 + 1) * dir) / TILE);
  const num3 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 1) / TILE); // 脚底行（gravDir=1）
  const num4 = Math.floor(b.h / TILE) + (b.h % TILE !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
  if (!st.inBounds(num2, num3) || num3 >= st.h - 40) return;
  const solidAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) && st.isSolid(x, y);       // 平台非 tileSolid → 不阻挡（vanilla 语义）
  const halfAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) && !!st.half[st.idx(x, y)];
  const slopeAt = (x: number, y: number): number =>
    st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.slope[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
  // flag（:3700-3708）：身体放入探柱（j=2..num4 上方行全净空）
  for (let j = 2; j <= num4; j++) if (solidAt(num2, num3 - j)) return;
  // flag2（:3710-3714）：后上方对角净空
  if (solidAt(num2 - dir, num3 - num4)) return;
  const centerX = b.x + b.w / 2;
  // flag3（:3725-3728）：脚上一格净空 / 面朝坡 / 半砖且其上净空
  {
    const s = slopeAt(num2, num3 - 1);
    const ok = !solidAt(num2, num3 - 1)
      || (s === 1 && centerX > num2 * TILE)
      || (s === 2 && centerX < num2 * TILE + TILE)
      || (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && !solidAt(num2, num3 - num4 - 1));
    if (!ok) return;
  }
  // flag4（:3730-3737）：落脚格实心（面朝坡且身体沉入）/ 或脚上一格是半砖
  {
    const fs = slopeAt(num2, num3);
    const topSlope = fs === 1 || fs === 2;
    const a = solidAt(num2, num3)
      && (!topSlope || (fs === 1 && centerX < num2 * TILE) || (fs === 2 && centerX > num2 * TILE + TILE))
      && (!topSlope || b.y + b.h > num3 * TILE);
    const bb = halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && solidAt(num2, num3 - 1);
    if (!(a || bb)) return;
  }
  // X 重叠门（:3745-3748）：探柱与移动后身体横向相交
  if (!(num2 * TILE < probeX + b.w && num2 * TILE + TILE > probeX)) return;
  // 抬升（:3750-3770）：半砖上一格 → rowTop-8；本格半砖 → rowTop+8；上限 16.1px
  let target = num3 * TILE;
  if (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1)) target -= 8;
  else if (halfAt(num2, num3)) target += 8;
321:export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {

/** 移动并碰撞。高速时分子步防穿透。 */
export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {
  // NPC Collision_MoveWhileWet（NPC.cs:93986）：wet 时位移 ×liquidMoveFactor（velocity 不变，
  // 只缩位移）。由 npcLiquid.updateNpcLiquid 每 tick 写入（dry=1 跳过）；实体基类缺省 1。
  const lf = b.liqFactor;
  if (lf !== undefined && lf !== 1) { dx *= lf; dy *= lf; }
  const wasGround = b.onGround;   // 上一 tick 落地态（StepDown 贴地门用——重置前捕获）
  b.onGround = false;
  b.hitWall = false;
  b.hitHead = false;
  // 原版 StepUp/StepDown 在 TileCollision 之前用未受阻的意图速度探测
  // （Player.cs:23252-23263 / NPC.cs:54377-54382）。StepDown 门：玩家 vy===GRAVITY
  // （原版字面 :23252）、NPC 族 onGround（原版 :54374 vy==0 在该时点=贴地语义；
  // 本仓重力先加,==0 恒假,取等价门）——宽门 vy>=0 会把低空飞行小动物周期性
  // 瞬移按压到地面（萤火虫/蝴蝶"怪力按地+闪现"根因）
  if (b.stepUp) {
    const gate = b.stepDownGate ?? 'grounded';
    if (gate === 'gravity' ? b.vy === GRAVITY : wasGround) applyStepDown(b, world);
    applyStepUp(b, world);
  }
  // 速度辅助（Collision.cs:1773-1790）：脚底中心所在格(原版为整行落地候选格的近似)
  // 是 slope1/2、水平速度朝低侧、脚已沉到对角线 → dy += |dx|
  // （等价 Velocity.Y += |Velocity.X|）把身体粘到斜面,消除坡顶角/高速下坡 1 帧的格顶滑行
  {
    const st = world.store;
    const feetRow = Math.floor((b.y + b.h) / TILE);
    const midCol = Math.floor((b.x + b.w / 2) / TILE);
    if (dx !== 0 && st.inBounds(midCol, feetRow)) {
      const fi = st.idx(midCol, feetRow);
      const fs = st.slope[fi];
      if ((fs === 1 || fs === 2) && !st.half[fi] && st.isSolid(midCol, feetRow)) {
        const vx0 = midCol * TILE;
        const num4 = fs === 1 ? b.x - vx0 : vx0 + TILE - (b.x + b.w);
        if (num4 >= 0 && b.y + b.h >= feetRow * TILE + num4
          && ((fs === 1 && b.vx > 0) || (fs === 2 && b.vx < 0))) {
          dy += Math.abs(dx);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  const maxStep = 8;
  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) / maxStep));
  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
    const sx = dx / steps, sy = dy / steps;
    const { hitX, hitY } = moveAxis(b, world, sx, sy);
    if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }
    if (hitY) dy = 0;
    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) break;
  }
  slopeCollide(b, world);
}

// 提取器自审计测试(锁 2026-08-13 "铜剑丢出去"机制=extract-itemcombat targetId 错基底):
// ① 常跑层:直接查已提交 JSON——3507/3509 钉死断言(错基底时必翻)+基数护栏
// ② 审计层:C# 源存在时 spawn --audit——anomalies 为空+pinned 与已提交 JSON deep equal
//    (同时抓"提取器坏但数据没重生成"和"数据被重生成坏"两个方向)
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import itemCombatJson from '../src/data/vanilla-itemcombat.json';
import projStatusJson from '../src/data/vanilla-projstatus.json';

const ROOT = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const CS_PRESENT = existsSync(join(ROOT, '..', 'Terarria1456', 'Terraria', 'Item.cs'));
const TABLE = itemCombatJson as unknown as Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>;

describe('提取器自审计·常跑层(已提交 JSON,不依赖 C# 源)', () => {
  it('3507 铜短剑钉死(bug#2 一行式回归锁):直刺投射物语义', () => {
    // 错取 targetId=1(铁镐)时:autoReuse:true 且无 shoot → 两条断言必翻其一
    const r = TABLE['3507'];
    expect(r.shoot).toBe(938);
    expect(r.noMelee).toBe(true);
    expect(r.autoReuse).toBe(false);
    expect(r.shootSpeed).toBe(2.1);
  });

  it('3509 铜镐钉死:纯工具不得带 shoot("丢出去"的另一面)', () => {
    const r = TABLE['3509'];
    expect('shoot' in r).toBe(false);
    expect(r.melee).toBe(true);
    expect(r.useTime).toBe(15);
  });

  it('基数护栏:提取器结构漂移后数据量骤降必翻', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(TABLE).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2500);
    let melee = 0;
    for (const v of Object.values(TABLE)) if (v.melee) melee++;
    expect(melee).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(250); // 实测 302;护栏取 80% 量级防骤降
  });

  it('projstatus 基数:88 型号/97 条基线(互斥组语义修正后)', () => {
    const entries = Object.values(projStatusJson as unknown as Record<string, unknown[]>)
      .reduce((n, v) => n + v.length, 0);
    expect(Object.keys(projStatusJson).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(85);
    expect(entries).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(95);
  });
});

// 审计层:C# 反编译源不存在(其他环境)时 warn 跳过,不阻塞
(CS_PRESENT ? describe : describe.skip)('提取器自审计·审计层(spawn --audit)', () => {
  const run = (tool: string): { stdout: string; status: number | null } => {
    const r = spawnSync('node', [join(ROOT, 'tools', tool), '--audit'], {
      cwd: ROOT, encoding: 'utf8', timeout: 120_000,
    });
    return { stdout: r.stdout ?? '', status: r.status };
  };

  it('extract-itemcombat:anomalies 为空 + pinned 3507/3509 与已提交 JSON deep equal', () => {
    const { stdout, status } = run('extract-itemcombat.mjs');
    const j = JSON.parse(stdout.trim().split('\n').pop()!);
    expect(j.anomalies).toEqual([]);
    expect(j.delegates).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(20); // X-04 基数
    expect(j.pinned['3507']).toEqual(TABLE['3507']);
    expect(j.pinned['3509']).toEqual(TABLE['3509']);
    void status;
  });

  it('extract-projstatus:跳过数 ≤ 基线(语义修正后 16,护栏 25)', () => {
    const { stdout } = run('extract-projstatus.mjs');
    // 该脚本 console.log 混入 stdout——取最后一行 JSON
    const line = stdout.trim().split('\n').pop()!;
    const j = JSON.parse(line);
    expect(j.types).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(85);
    expect(j.skipped).toBeLessThanOrEqual(25);
  });
});

//   原 vy>=0 宽门 → 低空飞行小动物被周期性瞬移按压（Collision.cs StepDown 7~17px 吸附）。
//   修复后门：NPC 族 = onGround（原版 NPC.cs:54374 vy==0 在该时点=贴地语义）、
//   玩家 = vy===GRAVITY（Player.cs:23252 字面）。空中实体（onGround=false）永不吸附。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { moveAndCollide } from '../src/physics/TileCollision';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { GRAVITY, TILE } from '../src/core/constants';

const W = 40, H = 40, FLOOR = 21;

function makeWorld(): World {
  const w = new World(W, H, 7, 'stepdown-test');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) st.setTile(x, FLOOR, 1);   // 全幅地板 row21
  return w;
}

/** 刚走下台阶的实体：全身悬于地板上方 16px（gap∈StepDown 7~17 窗），水平移动中 */
function body(over: Partial<{ onGround: boolean; vy: number; stepDownGate: 'grounded' | 'gravity' }>) {
  return {
    x: 12 * TILE, y: (FLOOR - 1) * TILE - 16,   // 脚底 336-16=320,距地板顶 16px
    w: 12, h: 16,
    vx: 2, vy: GRAVITY,
    onGround: true, hitWall: false, hitHead: false,
    stepUp: true,
    ...over,
  } as Parameters<typeof moveAndCollide>[0];
}

describe('StepDown 门（Collision.cs:3577-3638）', () => {
  it('贴地行走（onGround=true）→ 仍吸附下台阶（原行为保留）', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const b = body({});
    const yBefore = b.y;
    moveAndCollide(b, w, b.vx, b.vy);
    expect(b.y).toBeGreaterThan(yBefore + 8);   // 被 StepDown 瞬移吸附 16px 贴地
  });

  it('★空中飞行（onGround=false, vy=0.3 平飞）→ 不吸附（修复核心）', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const b = body({ onGround: false, vy: 0.3 });
    const yBefore = b.y;
    moveAndCollide(b, w, b.vx, b.vy);
    expect(b.y - yBefore).toBeLessThan(2);      // 只按 vy 缓降,无瞬移按压
  });

  it('玩家门 = vy===GRAVITY 字面：vy=GRAVITY 吸附、vy=0.3 不吸附', () => {
    const w = makeWorld();
    const a = body({ stepDownGate: 'gravity' as const, vy: GRAVITY });
    const y0 = a.y;
    moveAndCollide(a, w, a.vx, a.vy);
    expect(a.y).toBeGreaterThan(y0 + 8);        // vy==gravity → 吸附（Player.cs:23252）

    const w2 = makeWorld();
    const c = body({ stepDownGate: 'gravity' as const, vy: 0.3 });
    const y1 = c.y;
    moveAndCollide(c, w2, c.vx, c.vy);
    expect(c.y - y1).toBeLessThan(2);           // 空中缓降 → 不吸附
  });

```

guard.gate : null,\n  });\n}\nfunction makeGuard(kind, cond) {\n  const t = parseTypeCond(cond);\n  return {\n    kind, cond,\n    types: t.include, exclude: t.exclude.size ? t.exclude : null,\n    bounds: { lt: t.lt, lte: t.lte, gt: t.gt, gte: t.gte },\n    life: parseLife(cond), gate: parseGate(cond),\n  };\n}\n/** 调用点守卫链解析 → { types:Set|null, exclude:Set, life, gates:string[], typeCtxId } */\nfunction resolveChain(extraCase) {\n  const chain = [...stack];\n  if (extraCase) chain.push(extraCase);\n  let include = null, typeCtxId = null;\n  const exclude = new Set(), gates = [];\n  for (c.bounds.gt != null && include) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t > c.bounds.gt));\n      if (c.bounds.gte != null && include) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t >= c.bounds.gte));\n    }\n  }\n  // life：内层最近者优先\n  let life = null;\n  for (let i = chain.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (chain[i] && chain[i].life) { life = chain[i].life; break; }\n  }\n  return { types: include, exclude, life, gates, typeCtxId };\n}\n\nfunction recordLocalAssign(name, valExpr, line, typeCtxId) {\n  if (!locals.has(name)) locals.set(name, []);\n  locals.get(name).push({ line, valExpr, typeCtxId });\n}\n\n/** 局部变量按 type 还原 → Map<type, val> | {single} | null */\nfunction resolveLocal(name, callLine, typeCtxId, include) {\n  const hist = (locals.get(name) ?? []).filter((h) => h.line < callLine && h.typeCtxId === typeCtxId);\n  if (!hist.length) return null;\n  if (include) {\n    const perType = new Map();\n    for (const t of include) {\n      let v = null;\n      for (const h of hist) {\n        const e = evalIdExpr(h.valExpr);\n        if (!e) { v = undefined; break; } // 赋值本身不可求值\n        if (e.kind === 'id') v = e.id;\n        else if (e.kind === 'range') v = { min: e.min, max: e.max };\n        else if (e.kind === 'ids') v = e.ids;\n      }\n      if (v === undefined) return null;\n      if (v === null) return null;\n      perType.set(t, JSON.stringify(v));\n    }\n    const uniq = [...new Set(perType.values())];\n    if (uniq.length === 1) {\n      const p = JSON.parse(uniq[0]);\n      return Array.isArray(p) ? { kind: 'ids', ids: p } : typeof p === 'object' ? { kind: 'range', ...p } : { kind: 'id', id: p };\n    }\n    // 逐 type 不同值 → 展开\n    const per = new Map();\n    for (const [t, raw] of perType) per.set(t, JSON.parse(raw));\n    return { kind: 'perType', per };\n  }\n  const last = hist[hist.length - 1];\n  const e = evalIdExpr(last.valExpr);\n  return e ?? null;\n}\n\nfunction emitEntry(npcId, e, line, gates) {\n  if (!entries.has(npcId)) entries.set(npcId, []);\n  entries.get(npcId).push({ ...e, line, ...(gates.length ? { gates } : {}) });\n}\n\nfunction idFields(e, exprRaw) {\n  if (e.kind === 'id') return { id: e.id };\n  if (e.kind === 'range') return { idMin: e.min, idMax: e.max, n: e.max - e.min + 1 };\n  if (e.kind === 'ids') return { ids: e.ids, n: e.ids.length };\n  void exprRaw;\n  return {};\n}\n\n// ---------- 逐行扫描 ----------\nfor (let ln = headIdx + 1; ln < bodyEnd; ln++) {\n  const raw = src[ln];\n  let s = raw;\n  // 1) 前导闭括号：弹栈 + 清 case 标签\n  const closes = (s.match(/^\\}+/) || [''])[0].length;\n  if (closes) {\n    for (let k = 0; k < closes && stack.length; k++) stack.pop();\n    depth -= closes;\n    for (const k of [...caseAt.keys()]) if (k > depth) caseAt.delete(k);\n    s = s.slice(closes);\n  }\n  const trimmed = s.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) continue;\n\n  // 2) 局部变量赋值流（声明与重赋值）\n  {\n    let m;\n    if ((m = trimmed.match(/^(?:int|float|double|byte|short|long|uint|var|bool)\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*(.+?);$/))\n      || (m = trimmed.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=(?![=>])\\s*(.+?);$/))) {\n      if (!/^(if|for|while|switch|return|else)$/.test(m[1])) {\n        const r = resolveChain(null);\n        recordLocalAssign(m[1], m[2], ln + 1, r.typeCtxId);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 3) 守卫头解析\n  let guard = null, caseLabel = null;\n  {\n    let m;\n    if ((m = trimmed.match(/^(else\\s+if|if)\\s*\\(/))) guard = makeGuard(m[1].replace(' ', ''), parenBody(trimmed, m[0].length - 1));\n    else if (/^else\\s*(\\{|$)/.test(trimmed)) guard = makeGuard('else', 'else');\n    else if ((m = trimmed.match(/^switch\\s*\\(/))) guard = makeGuard('switch', parenBody(trimmed, m[0].length - 1));\n    else if ((m = trimmed.match(/^case\\s+(.+?):/))) caseLabel = m[1].trim();\n    else if (/^default\\s*:/.test(trimmed)) caseLabel = null, caseAt.set(depth, null);\n    else if (/^(for|foreach|while|do|try)\\b/.test(trimmed)) guard = { kind: 'loop', cond: null, types: null, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null, gate: null };\n  }\n  if (caseLabel !== null && caseLabel !== undefined) {\n    const e = evalIdExpr(caseLabel);\n    const g = e && e.kind === 'id' ? { label: caseLabel, types: new Set([e.id]) } : { label: caseLabel, types: null };\n    caseAt.set(depth, g);\n  }\n\n  // 4) 压栈（本行 '{' 或下一行独立 '{'）\n  const opens = (trimmed.match(/\\{/g) || []).length;\n  if (opens) {\n    for (let k = 0; {} : { posExpr };\n    const base = { ...velFields, ...scaleFields, ...posFields };\n\n    let e = evalIdExpr(idExpr);\n    let perType = null;\n    if (!e) {\n      const lv = idExpr.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)$/);\n      if (lv) {\n        const res = resolveLocal(lv[1], ln + 1, r.typeCtxId, r.types);\n        if (res && res.kind === 'perType') { perType = res.per; e = null; }\n        else if (res) e = res;\n      }\n    }\n\n    const target = r.types ? { ids: v, n: v.length } : typeof v === 'object' ? { idMin: v.min, idMax: v.max, n: v.max - v.min + 1 } : { id: v };\n        emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...f }, ln + 1, r.gates);\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (e) {\n      emitEntriesFor(target, { ...base, ...idFields(e) }, ln + 1, r.gates);\n      continue;\n    }\n    // 不可静态求值：保留表达式（挂到该 type 组，运行时跳过）\n    for (const t of target) emitEntry(t, { ...base, expr: idExpr }, ln + 1, r.gates);\n    unresolved.push({ line: ln + 1, expr: idExpr, types: target.length > 6 ? `${target[0]}..${target[target.length - 1]}(${target.length})` : target.join('|') });\n  }\n}\n\nfunction emitEntriesFor(types, fields, line, gates) {\n  for (const t of types) emitEntry(t, fields, line, gates);\n}\n\n// ============ 贴图尺寸表（出现过的 gore id） ============\nconst tex = {};\nconst idsSeen = new Set();\nfor (const list of entries.values()) {\n  for (const e of list) {\n    if (e.id != null) idsSeen.add(e.id);\n    if (e.idMin != null) for (let i = e.idMin; i <= (e.idMax ?? e.idMin); i++) idsSeen.add(i);\n    if (e.ids) for (const i of e.ids) idsSeen.add(i);\n  }\n}\nfor (const id of [...idsSeen].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {\n  const sz = pngSize(id);\n  if (sz) tex[id] = sz;\n}\n\n// ============ 落盘 ============\nconst out = { _meta: {\n  source: 'Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs HitEffect (:82421-:90873) + Gore.cs',\n  generatedBy: 'tools/extract-gore.mjs',\n  callsTotal,\n  callsHitSkipped,\n  callsPartySkipped,\n  npcIds: entries.size,\n  entryCount: [...entries.values()].reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0),\n  unresolvedCount: unresolved.length,\n  defaultEntryCount: defaultEntries.length,\n  unresolved,\n  defaultEntries: defaultEntries.slice(0, 40),\n  notes: [\n    'life>0 守卫段(hit gore)与派对帽段(UsesPartyHat)已剔除',\n    '无 life 守卫的调用在原版 hit/death 双段都会执行(HitEffect 死亡必调)→计为死亡 gore',\n    'X+Main.rand.Next(N) 静态展开为 idMin..idMax 区间,运行时均匀取值',\n    'velocity=velocity 表示继承 NPC 当前速度;NewGore 附加随机扰动见 Gore.cs:1347-1348(运行时)',\n  ],\n  tex,\n} };\nfor (const [k, v] of [...entries.entries()].sort((a, b) => (+a[0]) - (+b[0]))) out[k] = v;\n\nif (process.argv.includes('--audit')) {\n  console.log(JSON.stringify({\n    npcIds: entries.size, entryCount: out._meta.entryCount, callsTotal,\n    callsHitSkipped, callsPartySkipped, unresolved: unresolved.length,\n    defaultEntries: defaultEntries.length, texKnown: Object.keys(tex).length,\n    sample: { '1': out['1'], '3': out['3'] },\n  }));\n} else {\n  writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\\n');\n  console.log(`vanilla-gore.json: ${entries.size} npcIds, ${out._meta.entryCount} entries, `\n    + `${unresolved.length} unresolved, ${defaultEntries.length} default-less, tex ${Object.keys(tex).length}`);\n}\n"

s.match(/type\\s*>\\s*(\\d+)/))) res.gt = +m[1];\n  if ((m = s.match(/type\\s*>=\\s*(\\d+)/)) && +m[1] !== 0) res.gte = +m[1];\n  return res;\n}\n\nfunction parseLife(cond) {\n  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return null;\n  if (/life\\s*<=\\s*0/.test(cond)) return 'death';\n  if (/life\\s*>\\s*0/.test(cond) || /life\\s*>=\\s*1/.test(cond)) return 'alive';\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** `ai[N] == V` / `ai[N] != V` → 结构化门 */\nfunction parseAiGates(cond) {\n  if (!cond) return [];\n  const out = [];\n  for (const m of cond.matchAll(/ai\\[(\\d)\\]\\s*==\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?/g)) out.push({ slot: +m[1], value: +m[2], not: false });\n  for (const m of cond.matchAll(/ai\\[(\\d)\\]\\s*!=\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?/g)) out.push({ slot: +m[1], value: +m[2], not: true });\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** 剥掉 type/life/ai 谓词后剩余标识符 → 附加门（专家模式/变体等），无则 null */\nfunction parseGate(cond) {\n  if (!cond || cond === 'else') return null;\n  let s = cond;\n  s = s.replace(/type\\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\\s*(-?\\d+|\\w+(\\.\\w+)?)/g, ' ');\n  s = s.replace(/life\\s*(<=|>=|==|!=|>|<)\\s*[\\d.]+[fFdD]?/g, ' ');\n  s = s.replace(/ai\\[\\d\\]\\s*(==|!=)\\s*[\\d.]+[fF]?/g, ' ');\n  s = s.replace(/\\b(life|type|ai)\\b/g, ' ');\n  s = s.replace(/[\\d.]+[fFdD]?/g, ' ').replace(/[()[\\]{}&|!?:.,;=+\\-*/<>]/g, ' ');\n  const ids = [...new Set(s.split(/\\s+/).filter((t) => /^[A-Za-z_]/.test(t)))];\n  return ids.length ? ids.join('+') : null;\n}\n\n// ============ gore id 表达式求值 ============\nfunction evalIdExpr(expr) {\n  const s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+)[fFdD]?$/))) return { kind: 'id', id: +m[1] };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(\\w+)\\s*\\?\\s*(-?\\d+)\\s*:\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) {\n    // IsShimmerVariant ? 微光变体 : 常规 —— 常规态取 else 支\n    return { kind: 'id', id: +m[3], shimmerAlt: +m[2] };\n  }\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    const a = +m[1]; return { kind: 'range', min: a, max: a + +m[2] - 1 };\n  }\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(-?\\d+)\\s*\\+\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*,\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    const a = +m[1]; return { kind: 'range', min: a + +m[2], max: a + +m[3] - 1 };\n  }\n  if ((m = s.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*,\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) return { kind: 'range', min: +m[1], max: +m[2] - 1 };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(\\d+)\\s*\\)$/))) return { kind: 'range', min: 0, max: +m[1] - 1 };\n  if (/^Utils\\.SelectRandom</.test(s)) {\n    const ids = [...s.matchAll(/(\\d+)[fFdD]?/g)].map((x) => +x[1]);\n    if (ids.length) return { kind: 'ids', ids };\n  }\n  // 局部变量 ± 常数偏移（人形族 `num46 + 3` 头/臂/腿族）\n  if ((m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*\\+\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) return { kind: 'local', name: m[1], off: +m[2] };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*-\\s*(-?\\d+)$/))) return { kind: 'local', name: m[1], off: -+m[2] };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)$/))) return { kind: 'local', name: m[1], off: 0 };\n  return null;\n}\n\n// ============ 主流程 ============\nconst src = readFileSync(NPC_CS, 'utf8').split('\\n');\nconst headIdx = src.findIndex((l) => l.includes('public void HitEffect(int hitDirection'));\nif (headIdx < 0) { console.error('HitEffect not found in NPC.cs'); process.exit(1); }\n\n// 方法体括号切片：签名行 '{' 起深度扫描到配平 '}'（其后即 `public static int CountNPCS`）\nlet bodyEnd = headIdx;\n{\n  let d = 0, seen = false;\n  for (let i = headIdx; i < src.length; i++) {\n    for (const c of src[i]) {\n      if (c === '{') { d++; seen = true; } else if (c === '}') d--;\n    }\n    if (seen && d === 0) { bodyEnd = i; break; }\n  }\n}\n\n// 逻辑行：括号深度 >0 持续拼接（跨行实参）\nconst logical = [];\n{\n  let buf = null;\n  for (let ln = headIdx; ln <= bodyEnd; ln++) {\n    if (!buf) buf = { text: src[ln], line: ln + 1 };\n    else buf.text += ' ' + src[ln].trim();\n    if (parenDepth(buf.text) <= 0) { logical.push(buf); buf = null; }\n  }\n  if (buf) logical.push(buf);\n}\n\nconst stack = [];       // 深度栈：每项一个守卫上下文\nlet depth = 0;\nlet ctxSeq = 0;\nlet pendingGuard = null;\nconst caseAt = new Map(); // depth → { label, types:Set|null }\nconst locals = new Map(); // name → [{ line, valExpr, typeCtxId }]\n\nconst entries = new Map();\nconst defaultEntries = [];\nconst unresolved = [];\nlet callsTotal = 0, callsHitSkipped = 0, callsPartySkipped = 0;\n\nfunction makeGuard(kind, cond) {\n  const t = parseTypeCond(cond);\n  return {\n    kind, cond,\n    types: t.hasInclude ? t.include : null,\n    exclude: t.exclude.size ? t.exclude : null,\n    bounds: { lt: t.lt, lte: t.lte, gt: t.gt, gte: t.gte },\n    life: parseLife(cond),\n    gate: parseGate(cond),\n    ai: parseAiGates(cond),\n  };\n}\nfunction pushCtx(guard) {\n  ctxSeq++;\n  stack.push({\n    id: ctxSeq, kind: guard?.kind ?? null, cond: guard?.cond ?? null,\n    types: guard?.types ?? null, exclude: guard?.exclude ?? null,\n    bounds: guard?.bounds ?? null, life: guard?.life ?? null,\n    gate: guard?.gate ?? null, ai: guard?.ai ?? null,\n  });\n}\n/** 调用点守卫链解析 */\nfunction resolveChain(extraCase) {\n  const chain = [...stack];\n  if (extraCase) chain.push(extraCase);\n  let include = null, typeCtxId = null;\n  const exclude = new Set(), gates = [], ai = [];\n  for (const c of chain) {\n    if (!c) continue;\n    if (c.types) {\n      include = include ? new Set([...include].filter((t) => c.types.has(t))) : new Set(c.types);\n      typeCtxId = typeCtxId ?? c.id;\n    }\n    if (c.exclude) for (const t of c.exclude) exclude.add(t);\n    if (c.gate) gates.push(c.gate);\n    if (c.ai) ai.push(...c.ai);\n    if (c.bounds && include) {\n      if (c.bounds.lt != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t < c.bounds.lt));\n      if (c.bounds.lte != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t <= c.bounds.lte));\n      if (c.bounds.gt != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t > c.bounds.gt));\n      if (c.bounds.gte != null) include = new Set([...include].filter((t) => t >= c.bounds.gte));\n    }\n  }\n  let life = null;\n  for (let i = chain.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (chain[i]?.life) { life = chain[i].life; break; }\n  return { types: include, exclude, life, gates, ai, typeCtxId };\n}\n\nfunction recordLocalAssign(name, valExpr, line, typeCtxId) {\n  if (!locals.has(name)) locals.set(name, []);\n  locals.get(name).push({ line, valExpr, typeCtxId });\n}\n\n/** 局部变量按 type 还原 → {kind:id|range|ids|perType} | null */\nfunction resolveLocal(e, callLine, typeCtxId, include) {\n  const hist = (locals.get(e.name) ?? []).filter((h) => h.line < callLine && h.typeCtxId === typeCtxId);\n  if (!hist.length) return null;\n  const shift = (v) => {\n    if (v == null) return null;\n    if (v.kind === 'id') return { kind: 'id', id: v.id + e.off };\n    if (v.kind === 'range') return { kind: 'range', min: v.min + e.off, max: v.max + e.off };\n    if (v.kind === 'ids') return { kind: 'ids', ids: v.ids.map((i) => i + e.off) };\n    return null;\n  };\n  if (include) {\n    const perType = new Map();\n    for (const t of include) {\n      let v = null;\n      for (const h of hist) {\n        const hv = evalIdExpr(h.valExpr);\n        if (!hv || hv.kind === 'local' || hv.kind === undefined) { v = null; break; }\n        v = shift(hv);\n      }\n      if (!v) return null;\n      perType.set(t, JSON.stringify(v));\n    }\n    const uniq = [...new Set(perType.values())];\n    if (uniq.length === 1) return JSON.parse(uniq[0]);\n    const per = new Map();\n    for (const [t, raw] of perType) per.set(t, JSON.parse(raw));\n    return { kind: 'perType', per };\n  }\n  const hv = evalIdExpr(hist[hist.length - 1].valExpr);\n  if (!hv || hv.kind === 'local') return null;\n  return shift(hv);\n}\n\nfunction idFields(e) {\n  if (e.kind === 'id') return { id: e.id };\n  if (e.kind === 'range') return { idMin: e.min, idMax: e.max, n: e.max - e.min + 1 };\n  if (e.kind === 'ids') return { ids: e.ids, n: e.ids.length };\n  return {};\n}\n\nfunction emitEntry(npcId, fields, line, gates, ai) {\n  if (!entries.has(npcId)) entries.set(npcId, []);\n  const f = { ...fields, line };\n  if (gates.length) f.gates = gates;\n  if (ai.length) f.ai = ai;\n  entries.get(npcId).push(f);\n}\n\n// ---------- 逻辑行扫描 ----------\nfor (const { text: raw0, line: lineNo } of logical) {\n  let s = raw0;\n  // 1) 前导闭括号（跳过缩进）：弹栈 + 清 case 标签\n  const mClose = s.match(/^\\s*\\}+/);\n  const closes = mClose ? mClose[0].trim().length : 0;\n  if (closes) {\n    for (let k = 0; k < closes && stack.length; k++) stack.pop();\n    depth -= closes;\n    for (const k of [...caseAt.keys()]) if (k > depth) caseAt.delete(k);\n    s = s.slice(mClose[0].length);\n  }\n  const trimmed = s.trim();\n  if (!trimmed) continue;\n\n  // 2) 局部变量赋值流（声明与重赋值；供 `num + K` id 还原）\n  {\n    let m;\n    if ((m = trimmed.match(/^(?:int|float|double|byte|short|long|uint|var|bool|Vector2)\\s+([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=\\s*(.+?);$/))\n      || (m = trimmed.match(/^([A-Za-z_]\\w*)\\s*=(?![=>])\\s*(.+?);$/))) {\n      if (!/^(if|for|while|switch|return|else)$/.test(m[1])) {\n        const r = resolveChain(null);\n        recordLocalAssign(m[1], m[2], lineNo, r.typeCtxId);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 3) 守卫头解析\n  let guard = null, caseLabel = undefined;\n  {\n    let m;\n    if ((m = trimmed.match(/^(else\\s+if|if)\\s*\\(/))) guard = makeGuard(m[1].replace(/\\s+/g, ''), parenBody(trimmed, m[0].length - 1));\n    else if (/^else(\\s|$)/.test(trimmed)) guard = makeGuard('else', 'else');\n    else if ((m = trimmed.match(/^switch\\s*\\(/))) guard = makeGuard('switch', parenBody(trimmed, m[0].length - 1));\n    else if ((m = trimmed.match(/^case\\s+(.+?):/))) caseLabel = m[1].trim();\n    else if (/^default\\s*:/.test(trimmed)) caseAt.set(depth, null);\n    else if (/^(for|foreach|while|do|try)\\b/.test(trimmed)) guard = { kind: 'loop', cond: null, types: null, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null, gate: null, ai: null };\n  }\n  if (caseLabel !== undefined) {\n    const e = evalIdExpr(caseLabel);\n    caseAt.set(depth, { label: caseLabel, types: e && e.kind === 'id' ? new Set([e.id]) : null });\n  }\n\n  // 4) 压栈（本行 '{'；守卫行无 '{' 时挂 pending 等下一行）\n  const opens = (trimmed.match(/\\{/g) || []).length;\n  if (opens) {\n    for (let k = 0; k < opens; k++) pushCtx(k === 0 ? (guard ?? pendingGuard) : null);\n    depth += opens;\n    pendingGuard = null;\n  } else if (guard) {\n    pendingGuard = guard;\n  }\n\n  // 5) Gore.NewGore / NewGorePerfect / NewGoreDirect 调用\n  let searchFrom = 0;\n  for (;;) {\n    const at = trimmed.indexOf('Gore.NewGore', searchFrom);\n    if (at < 0) break;\n    searchFrom = at + 1;\n    callsTotal++;\n    const p = trimmed.indexOf('(', at);\n    if (p < 0) continue;\n    const callee = trimmed.slice(at, p);\n    if (!/^Gore\\.NewGore(Perfect|Direct)?$/.test(callee)) continue;\n    const a = namedArgs(splitArgs(parenBody(trimmed, p)));\n    if (a.Position === undefined || a.Type === undefined) continue;\n    const cc = caseAt.get(depth) ?? caseAt.get(depth - 1) ?? null;\n    const r = resolveChain(cc ? { id: 0, kind: 'case', cond: `case ${cc.label}`, types: cc.types, exclude: null, bounds: null, life: null, gate: null, ai: null } : null);\n\n    if (r.gates.some((g) => g.includes('UsesPartyHat'))) { callsPartySkipped++; continue; } // 派对帽专属\n    if (r.life === 'alive') { callsHitSkipped++; continue; }                               // hit 段\n\n    const velFields = (() => {\n      const v = String(a.Velocity ?? 'velocity');\n      if (v === 'velocity') return {};\n      if (v === 'Vector2.Zero') return { vx: 0, vy: 0 };\n      let m;\n      if ((m = v.match(/^new Vector2\\(\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?\\s*,\\s*(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?\\s*\\)$/))) return { vx: +m[1], vy: +m[2] };\n      return { velExpr: v };\n    })();\n    const scaleFields = (() => {\n      const v = a.Scale;\n      if (v === undefined || v === '1f' || v === '1') return {};\n      let m;\n      if ((m = String(v).match(/^([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { scale: +m[1] };\n      return { scaleExpr: String(v) };\n    })();\n    const posFields = a.Position === 'position' ? {} : { posExpr: String(a.Position) };\n    const perfect = callee === 'Gore.NewGorePerfect' ? { perfect: true } : {};\n    const base = { ...velFields, ...scaleFields, ...posFields, ...perfect };\n\n    let e = evalIdExpr(a.Type);\n    let perType = null;\n    if (e && e.kind === 'local') {\n      const res = resolveLocal(e, lineNo, r.typeCtxId, r.types);\n      if (res?.kind === 'perType') perType = res.per;\n      else if (res) e = res;\n      else e = null;\n    } else if (e && e.kind !== 'id' && e.kind !== 'range' && e.kind !== 'ids') {\n      e = null;\n    }\n\n    if (!r.types) {\n      defaultEntries.push({ line: lineNo, idExpr: String(a.Type), gates: r.gates });\n      continue;\n    }\n    const target = [...r.types].filter((t) => !r.exclude.has(t));\n\n    if (perType) {\n      for (const t of target) {\n        const v = perType.get(t);\n        const f = Array.isArray(v) ? { ids: v, n: v.length }\n          : typeof v === 'object' ? { idMin: v.min, idMax: v.max, n: v.max - v.min + 1 }\n            : { id: v };\n        emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...f }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai);\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (e && (e.kind === 'id' || e.kind === 'range' || e.kind === 'ids')) {\n      for (const t of target) emitEntry(t, { ...base, ...idFields(e) }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai);\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const t of target) emitEntry(t, { ...base, expr: String(a.Type) }, lineNo, r.gates, r.ai);\n    unresolved.push({\n      line: lineNo, expr: String(a.Type),\n      types: target.length > 6 ? `${target[0]}..${target[target.length - 1]}(${target.length})` : target.join('|'),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// ============ 贴图尺寸表（出现过的 gore id） ============\nconst idsSeen = new Set();\nfor (const list of entries.values()) {\n  for (const e of list) {\n    if (e.id != null) idsSeen.add(e.id);\n    if (e.idMin != null) for (let i = e.idMin; i <= (e.idMax ?? e.idMin); i++) idsSeen.add(i);\n    if (e.ids) for (const i of e.ids) idsSeen.add(i);\n  }\n}\nconst tex = {};\nfor (const id of [...idsSeen].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {\n  const sz = pngSize(id);\n  if (sz) tex[id] = sz;\n}\n\n// ============ 落盘 ============\nconst entryCount = [...entries.values()].reduce((n, l) => n + l.length, 0);\nconst out = {\n  _meta: {\n    source: 'Terarria1456/Terraria/NPC.cs HitEffect (:82421-:90873)；物理=Terraria/Gore.cs',\n    generatedBy: 'tools/extract-gore.mjs',\n    callsTotal,\n    callsHitSkipped,\n    callsPartySkipped,\n    npcIds: entries.size,\n    entryCount,\n    unresolvedCount: unresolved.length,\n    defaultEntryCount: defaultEntries.length,\n    unresolved,\n    defaultEntries: defaultEntries.slice(0, 40),\n    notes: [\n      'life>0 守卫段(hit gore)与派对帽段(UsesPartyHat)已剔除；无 life 守卫的调用原版 hit/death 双段都执行 → 计为死亡 gore',\n      'idMin..idMax = 原版 X+Next(N)/Next(A,B) 区间，运行时均匀取值；ids = SelectRandom 候选',\n      'velocity 缺省=继承 NPC 当前速度；NewGore 附加随机扰动见 Gore.cs:1347-1348（运行时实现）',\n      'scaleExpr=\"scale\" = 取 NPC.scale；posExpr 为原版位置表达式（默认 position=左上角）',\n      'ai=[{slot,value}] = 原版 ai[N]==V 门（运行时按 NPC ai 槽位判定，不满足跳过该条）',\n      'perfect=true = Gore.NewGorePerfect：随机扰动后回写精确 pos/vel（运行时不抖动）',\n      'gates = 其余不可求值守卫（专家模式/变体等），仅登记不拦截',\n      'tex = gore 贴图 {w,h}（IHDR 直读）；缺图 id 运行时回退 32px 碰撞盒（Gore.cs:999）',\n    ],\n    tex,\n  },\n};\nfor (const [k, v] of [...entries.entries()].sort((a, b) => (+a[0]) - (+b[0]))) out[k] = v;\n\nif (process.argv.includes('--audit')) {\n  console.log(JSON.stringify({\n    npcIds: entries.size, entryCount, callsTotal, callsHitSkipped, callsPartySkipped,\n    unresolved: unresolved.length, defaultEntries: defaultEntries.length,\n    texKnown: Object.keys(tex).length, texMissing: idsSeen.size - Object.keys(tex).length,\n    sample: { 1: out[1], 3: out[3], 50: out[50] },\n  }));\n} else {\n  writeFileSync(OUT, JSON.stringify(out, null, 1) + '\\n');\n  console.log(`vanilla-gore.json: ${entries.size} npcIds, ${entryCount} entries, `\n    + `${unresolved.length} unresolved, ${defaultEntries.length} default-less, tex ${Object.keys(tex).length}/${idsSeen.size}`);\n}\n"

Position patterns are very regular:
- `new Vector2(position.X <op A>, position.Y <op B>)` — with A/B being literals or `(float)(width/2)`-style body-relative terms.
- `base.Center` / `base.Top` / `base.Center + new Vector2(A, B)`.

Velocity patterns:
- `velocity * 0.8f` etc → npc velocity scaled.
- `new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` → (kb dir, 0).
- `default(Vector2)` → (0,0).
- `new Vector2((float)Main.rand.Next(-30, 31) * 0.2f, ...)` → random.
- `vector9` (a local Vector2!) — 9 occurrences unresolvable → treat as npc velocity fallback.

Rather than runtime-interpreting 122 patterns, better: **compile the posExpr/velExpr to a normalized form in the extractor**! Emit `{px, py}` offsets in "body units": e.g.:
- position.X/Y with literal offsets → `{ox: 0, oy: 20}` meaning pos = (x + ox, y + oy).
- Terms referencing width/height → express as fractions: `(float)(width / 2)` → `{oxF: 0.5}` (multiplier on npc width).

I'll implement a small normalizer in the extractor that converts posExpr into `{ox, oy, fx, fy}` (literal offset + body fraction multiplier), plus a `posKind` for center/top anchors:
- `new Vector2(position.X ± A, position.Y ± B)` → ox/oy literal; terms like `(float)(width / 2)` or `(float)width` → fx = 0.5 or 1.
- `base.Center` → anchor 'center' (ox=−w/2? No—gore position = npc.Center, i.e. x + w/2). Represent: anchor center = (fx 0.5, fy 0.5).
- `base.Center + new Vector2(A, B)` → center + offset.
- `base.Top` → (fx 0.5, fy 0).
- `Main.rand.Next(width)` → random fraction: `rx: true` → x = npc.x + rand()*w. Represent fx range [0,1] random: `fxRand: true`.
- Fallback: if unparseable → drop posExpr to anchor top-left (ox=oy=0)? No — keep `posExpr` raw for audit and default to (0,0) offset... Hmm, better to keep posExpr and let runtime do a *simple* interpretation with the same normalizer. But duplicating the parser in TS is wasteful.

Cleanest: extractor compiles to numeric fields:
```json
{ "off": [ox, oy], "frac": [fx, fy], "randFrac": [rx, ry], "anchor": "topleft"|"center"|"top" }
```
spawn pos = anchorPos + off + frac*[w,h] + (randFrac ? rand*[w,h] : 0). Runtime does one formula. 

Similarly velocity: compile to `{vx, vy, kx, ky, randX, randY, hitDir}`:
- `velocity` → kx=ky=1
- `velocity * 0.8f` → k=0.8
- `default(Vector2)`/`Vector2.Zero` → 0,0
- `new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` → hitDirX = 1 (vx = hitDirection)
- `new Vector2((float)Main.rand.Next(-30, 31) * 0.2f, ...)` → randX: 6.0 (±30*0.2) — represent as `randAmp: [6, 6]` meaning uniform(-6, 6). General: `Main.rand.Next(A, B) * M` → uniform(A*M, (B-1)*M).
- `new Vector2(Main.rand.Next(-2, 3), ...)` → uniform(-2, 2).
- fallback `vector9` → kx=ky=1 (npc velocity) + keep velExpr for audit.

This moves all interpretation to extract-time (static, testable) and keeps the runtime trivial. 

Let me now write the normalizer functions in the extractor:

```js
function compilePos(expr) → { off:[x,y], frac:[x,y], rand:[bool,bool], anchor } | null
function compileVel(expr) → { vx,vy, k:[x,y], randAmp:[x,y], hitDirX:bool } | null
```

Pos parsing details. Patterns (from survey):
- `new Vector2(position.X, position.Y + 20f)`
- `new Vector2(position.X + 14f, position.Y)`
- `new Vector2(position.X + (float)Main.rand.Next(width), position.Y + (float)Main.rand.Next(height))`
- `new Vector2(position.X + (float)(width / 2) - 24f, position.Y + (float)(height / 2) - 24f)`
- `new Vector2(position.X, position.Y + (float)(height / 2) - 15f)`
- `new Vector2(position.X, position.Y + (float)height - 20f)`
- `base.Center`, `base.Top`, `base.Bottom`?
- `base.Center + new Vector2(4f, 0f)`, `base.Center + new Vector2(-40f, -height / 2)`  ← note `- height / 2` (not (float)(height/2))!
- `new Vector2(base.Center.X, ...)`? maybe.
- `position + new Vector2(0f, 0f)` (seen at 83203: `position + new Vector2(0f, 0f)`).
- `Center`? 

Component grammar (per axis):
```
term := 'position.X' | 'position.Y' | 'base.Center.X' | 'base.Center.Y' | 'base.Top...' | number | '(float)(width / 2)' | '(float)width' | 'width / 2' | 'height' | 'Main.rand.Next(width)' | ...
expr := term (± term)*
```
Anchor resolution: if any term is base.Center.X → anchor center (frac 0.5 on x); base.Top.X? etc. Simplify: preprocess the expr string: replace `base.Center.X` → `position.X + (float)(width / 2)`, `base.Center.Y` → `position.Y + (float)(height / 2)`, `base.Top.X` → `position.X + (float)(width / 2)`, `base.Top.Y` → `position.Y`, `base.Bottom.X` → `position.X + (float)(width/2)`, `base.Bottom.Y` → `position.Y + (float)height`. Then everything reduces to `new Vector2(<x-expr>, <y-expr>)` or `position + new Vector2(a, b)` (→ x = position.X + a).

Then per-axis parse: split on the anchor `position.X`/`position.Y` and parse the remaining offset terms:
- `(float)(width / 2)` → frac 0.5; `(float)width` → 1; `width / 2` → 0.5; `(float)(height / 2)` → 0.5; `height` → 1; `Main.rand.Next(width)` → randFrac 1; `Main.rand.Next(height)` → randFrac 1.
- numeric literals (with f suffix) → off.
- Products like `height / 2`, `width * 0.5f`? Add `(\w+) / (\d+)` → frac = 1/n; `(\w+) \* ([\d.]+)f?` → frac = k.
- Everything else → fail (keep raw expr, default top-left offset 0).

The extractor then emits:
```json
"pos": { "x": [off, frac, rand?], "y": [...] }   // compact arrays? 
```
Readable: `pos: { ox, oy, fx, fy, rx, ry }` omitting zeros. Runtime: `px = npc.x + ox + fx*w + (rx ? Math.random()*w : 0)`.

Velocity compile: 
- normalize `velocity * 0.8f` → k 0.8; `velocity * 0f` → 0; `velocity` → 1.
- `new Vector2(a, b)` components: literals → vx/vy; `hitDirection` → hd=1; `Main.rand.Next(A, B) * M` → rand uniform; `Main.rand.Next(A)` → uniform(0, A-1); `Main.rand.NextFloat() * M`? etc. `default(Vector2)`/`Vector2.Zero` → 0,0.
- fallback → k=[1,1].

Let me now also double check: `velocity * 0f` → zero velocity. And what about `new Vector2(Vector2.UnitX...)`? rare.

 "new_string": "function idFields(e) {\n  if (e.kind === 'id') return { id: e.id };\n  if (e.kind === 'range') return { idMin: e.min, idMax: e.max, n: e.max - e.min + 1 };\n  if (e.kind === 'ids') return { ids: e.ids, n: e.ids.length };\n  return {};\n}\n\n// ---- 位置/速度表达式 → 结构化数值（运行时只做一次线性组合）----\n/** 单轴项求值 → { off, frac, rand } | null；axisDim = 'width' | 'height' */\nfunction axisTerm(term, axisDim) {\n  const t = term.trim();\n  let m;\n  if ((m = t.match(/^(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { off: +m[1] };\n  if ((m = t.match(/^\\(float\\)\\((width|height)\\s*\\/\\s*(\\d+)\\)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^(width|height)\\s*\\/\\s*(\\d+)$/))) {\n    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: 1 / +m[2] } : null;\n  }\n  if ((m = t.match(/^\\(float\\)(width|height)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^(width|height)$/))) {\n    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: 1 } : null;\n  }\n  if ((m = t.match(/^\\(?float\\)?\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(width|height)\\s*\\)$/)) || (m = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\(\\s*(width|height)\\s*\\)$/))) {\n    return m[1] === axisDim ? { rand: 1 } : null;\n  }\n  if ((m = t.match(/^(width|height)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n    return m[1] === axisDim ? { frac: +m[2] } : null;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 轴表达式（已剥 anchor）→ { off, frac, rand } | null */\nfunction axisExpr(expr, axisDim) {\n  let off = 0, frac = 0, rand = 0;\n  // 顶层 ± 切分（无括号嵌套数字场景）\n  const parts = expr.replace(/^\\s*\\+/, '').split(/\\s*\\+\\s*/);\n  const terms = [];\n  for (const p of parts) {\n    const neg = p.split(/\\s*-\\s*/);\n    for (let k = 0; k < neg.length; k++) {\n      if (neg[k] === '') continue;\n      terms.push({ t: neg[k], sign: k === 0 ? 1 : -1 });\n    }\n  }\n  for (const { t, sign } of terms) {\n    const r = axisTerm(t, axisDim);\n    if (!r) return null;\n    if (r.off != null) off += sign * r.off;\n    if (r.frac != null) frac += sign * r.frac;\n    if (r.rand != null) rand += sign * r.rand;\n  }\n  const out = {};\n  if (off) out.off = +off.toFixed(4);\n  if (frac) out.frac = +frac.toFixed(4);\n  if (rand) out.rand = 1;\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** Position 实参 → { ox,oy,fx,fy,rx,ry } | null（基准=position 左上角） */\nfunction compilePos(expr) {\n  let s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  // anchor 归一到 position.X/Y\n  s = s.replace(/base\\.Center\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Center\\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)(height / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Top\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Top\\.Y/g, 'position.Y')\n    .replace(/base\\.Bottom\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Bottom\\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)height');\n  let sx = null, sy = null;\n  if ((m = s.match(/^new Vector2\\((.*),\\s*(.*)\\)$/s))) { sx = m[1]; sy = m[2]; }\n  else if ((m = s.match(/^position\\s*\\+\\s*new Vector2\\((.*),\\s*(.*)\\)$/s))) { sx = `position.X + ${m[1]}`; sy = `position.Y + ${m[2]}`; }\n  else if (s === 'position') { return {}; }\n  else if (s === 'base.Center') { return { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 }; }\n  else if (s === 'base.Top') { return { fx: 0.5 }; }\n  else if (s === 'base.Bottom') { return { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 }; }\n  else return null;\n  const out = {};\n  for (const [axis, dim, e] of [['x', 'width', sx], ['y', 'height', sy]]) {\n    let body = e;\n    const am = body.match(new RegExp(`position\\\\.${axis}\\\\b`));\n    if (!am) return null;\n    body = body.replace(new RegExp(`position\\\\.${axis}\\\\b`), '');\n    const r = axisExpr(body, dim);\n    if (!r) return null;\n    if (r.off != null) out[`o${axis}`] = r.off;\n    if (r.frac != null) out[`f${axis}`] = r.frac;\n    if (r.rand) out[`r${axis}`] = 1;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** 速度实参 → { vx,vy,kx,ky,ax,ay,hd } | null（k=NPC 速度系数；a=均匀随机幅；\n *  hd=1 表示 vx 击退方向）。缺省（'velocity'）→ 全继承 */\nfunction compileVel(expr) {\n  const s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  if (s === 'velocity') return {};\n  if (s === 'Vector2.Zero' || s === 'default(Vector2)') return { vx: 0, vy: 0 };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^velocity\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return { kx: +m[1], ky: +m[1] };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^velocity\\s*\\*\\s*0f?$/))) return { vx: 0, vy: 0 };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^new Vector2\\((.*),\\s*(.*)\\)$/s))) {\n    const out = {};\n    const comp = (c, axis) => {\n      const t = c.trim();\n      let mm;\n      if ((mm = t.match(/^(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) out[`v${axis}`] = +mm[1];\n      else if (t === 'hitDirection') out.hd = 1;\n      else if ((mm = t.match(/^\\(?float\\)?\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +(((+mm[2] - 1) * +mm[3]) - (+mm[1] * +mm[3])) / 2;\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +mm[1] * +mm[3]; // 均匀区间下界\n      } else if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[2] - 1 - +mm[1]) / 2);\n        out[`m${axis}`] = +mm[1];\n      } else if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n        out[`a${axis}`] = +(Math.max(0, +mm[1] - 1) / 2);\n        out[`m${axis}`] = 0;\n      } else return false;\n      return true;\n    };\n    if (!comp(m[1], 'x') || !comp(m[2], 'y')) return null;\n    return out;\n  }\n  return null; // 不可编译（局部 Vector2 等）→ 运行时按继承 NPC 速度兜底\n}"
}

// 不可编译（局部 Vector2 等）→ 运行时按继承 NPC 速度兜底\n}",
 "new_string": "/** Position 实参 → { ox,oy,fx,fy,rx,ry } | null（基准=position 左上角） */\nfunction compilePos(expr) {\n  let s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  // anchor 前缀（base.Center/Top/Bottom/position + …）\n  let base = {};\n  if ((m = s.match(/^(base\\.Center|base\\.Top|base\\.Bottom|position)\\s*\\+\\s*(.+)$/s))) {\n    if (m[1] === 'base.Center') base = { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 };\n    else if (m[1] === 'base.Top') base = { fx: 0.5 };\n    else if (m[1] === 'base.Bottom') base = { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 };\n    s = m[2].trim();\n  }\n  // 实体参照归一到 position.X/Y + 尺寸项\n  s = s.replace(/base\\.Center\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Center\\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)(height / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Top\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Top\\.Y/g, 'position.Y')\n    .replace(/base\\.Bottom\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)(width / 2)')\n    .replace(/base\\.Bottom\\.Y/g, 'position.Y + (float)height')\n    .replace(/base\\.Right\\.X/g, 'position.X + (float)width')\n    .replace(/base\\.Left\\.X/g, 'position.X')\n    .replace(/base\\.Size\\s*\\*\\s*0\\.5f?/g, 'new Vector2((float)(width / 2), (float)(height / 2))');\n  if (s === 'position' || s === '') return { ...base };\n  if (s === 'base.Center') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 0.5 };\n  if (s === 'base.Top') return { fx: 0.5 };\n  if (s === 'base.Bottom') return { fx: 0.5, fy: 1 };\n  let sx = null, sy = null;\n  if (s.startsWith('new Vector2(')) {\n    const comps = splitArgs(parenBody(s, s.indexOf('(')));\n    if (comps.length !== 2) return null;\n    sx = comps[0]; sy = comps[1];\n  } else if ((m = s.match(/^(.*?)[+-]\\s*new Vector2\\(/s))) {\n    return null; // 复合表达式（vector19 链等）→ 留原文\n  } else {\n    return null;\n  }\n  const out = { ...base };\n  for (const [axisName, dim, comp] of [['x', 'width', sx], ['y', 'height', sy]]) {\n    let body = comp.trim();\n    const anchorRe = new RegExp(`position\\\\.${axisName.toUpperCase()}\\\\b`);\n    if (!anchorRe.test(body)) {\n      // 无 position 锚点：整体当作偏移项（含 width/height 项）\n      const r = axisExpr(body, dim);\n      if (!r) return null;\n      if (r.off != null) out[`o${axisName}`] = r.off;\n      if (r.frac != null) out[`f${axisName}`] = r.frac;\n      if (r.rand) out[`r${axisName}`] = 1;\n      continue;\n    }\n    body = body.replace(anchorRe, '');\n    const r = axisExpr(body, dim);\n    if (!r) return null;\n    if (r.off != null) out[`o${axisName}`] = r.off;\n    if (r.frac != null) out[`f${axisName}`] = r.frac;\n    if (r.rand) out[`r${axisName}`] = 1;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/** 速度实参 → { vx,vy,kx,ky,ax,ay,mx,my,hd } | null（k=NPC 速度系数；\n *  v=常量；m+a=均匀随机区间 [m-a, m+a]；hd=1 表示 vx 取击退方向） */\nfunction compileVel(expr) {\n  const s = String(expr).trim();\n  let m;\n  if (s === 'velocity') return {};\n  if (s === 'Vector2.Zero' || s === 'default(Vector2)') return { vx: 0, vy: 0 };\n  if ((m = s.match(/^velocity\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) return +m[1] === 0 ? { vx: 0, vy: 0 } : { kx: +m[1], ky: +m[1] };\n  if (!s.startsWith('new Vector2(')) return null;\n  const comps = splitArgs(parenBody(s, s.indexOf('(')));\n  if (comps.length !== 2) return null;\n  const out = {};\n  const comp = (c, axis) => {\n    const t = c.trim();\n    let mm;\n    if ((mm = t.match(/^(-?[\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) { out[`v${axis}`] = +mm[1]; return true; }\n    if (t === 'hitDirection') { out.hd = 1; return true; }\n    if ((mm = t.match(/^\\(float\\)\\s*Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))\n      || (mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n      const lo = +mm[1] * +mm[3], hi = (+mm[2] - 1) * +mm[3];\n      out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      return true;\n    }\n    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+),\\s*(-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n      const lo = +mm[1], hi = +mm[2] - 1;\n      out[`m${axis}`] = +((lo + hi) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      out[`a${axis}`] = +((hi - lo) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      return true;\n    }\n    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.Next\\((-?\\d+)\\)$/))) {\n      out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1] - 1) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      return true;\n    }\n    if ((mm = t.match(/^Main\\.rand\\.NextFloat\\(\\)\\s*\\*\\s*([\\d.]+)[fF]?$/))) {\n      out[`m${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      out[`a${axis}`] = +((+mm[1]) / 2).toFixed(4);\n      return true;\n    }\n    return false;\n  };\n  if (!comp(comps[0], 'x') || !comp(comps[1], 'y')) return null;\n  return out;\n}"
}

So for my families:
- default sticky chunk: gravity+rotation, then sticky collision+friction+lifetime, then position += velocity.
- blood shrink (non-sticky): shrink, then alpha += 2, then position += velocity.
- 276-282 (sticky): damp, gravity+rotation, sticky collision, position += velocity.
