- 1218 (SA7): early return in the switch → only sparks update.

moveAndCollide note: it takes (b, world, dx, dy) and mutates b.x/b.y/b.vx/b.vy. For gore, vanilla passes velocity as the delta and gets back the CLIPPED velocity. moveAndCollide zeroes b.vx/b.vy on collision — equivalent. One subtlety: `velocity = Collision.TileCollision(position, velocity, size, size)` uses the ORIGINAL velocity each call (not accumulated gravity?). We do gravity first then collision with the new velocity ✓.

Another subtlety: moveAndCollide sets onGround/hitWall/hitHead on the body ✓ harmless. And Entity has stepUp undefined → no step-up ✓ (gore shouldn't step up).

Velocity check `if (velocity.Y == 0f)` — after moveAndCollide, vy is exactly 0 on ground hit ✓ (float arithmetic in TS: vy = 0 exactly when hit).

static spawn(type, x, y, vx, vy, scale=1): GorePiece | null {

  if (goreTime===0 || NON_STICKY.has(type)) p.sticky = false;
  else if (375<=type<=377) { p.sticky=false; p.alpha=100; }
  else { p.sticky = true; p.timeLeft = GORE_TIME; }
  // 1217/1218 → Frame(1,3)
  ...
}

export function spawnNpcDeathGore(e: EnemyLike, game: GameHooks): number {
  if (e.vanillaId == null) return 0;
  const list = deathGoreFor(e.vanillaId);
  if (!list.length) return 0;
  const add = (game as unknown as { entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities;
  let n = 0;
  for (const entry of list) {
    if (entry.ai && !entry.ai.every(g => (e as any)['ai'+g.slot] === g.value !== !!g.not)) continue;
    ... resolve id/pos/vel/scale ...
    const p = GorePiece.spawn(...); if (p) { add?.add?.(p, 'props'); n++; }
  }
  return n;
}

1） */\nconst DISAPPEAR_ALPHA: Record<number, number> = { 1007: 3, 1008: 10, 1024: 7, 1025: 7, 1026: 7, 1218: 3 };\nconst vanishSpeed = (t: number) => DISAPPEAR_SPEED[t] ?? 1;\nconst vanishAlpha = (t: n?: number;\n  /** 常量速度 */\n  vx?: number; vy?: number;\n  /** NPC 速度系数（velocity * k） */\n  kx?: number; ky?: number;\n  /** 均匀随机速度 [m-a, m+a]（Main.rand.Next 区间编译产物） */\n  mx?: number; my?: number; ax?: number; ay?: number;\n  /** vx 取击退方向（new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)） */\n  hd?: number;\n  /** 出生点 = npc 左上角 + off + frac*[w,h] + rand*[w,h] */\n  ox?: number; oy?: number; fx?: number; fy?: number; rx?: number; ry?: number;\n  scale?: number; scaleExpr?: string;\n  /** 未编译的原文（登记用） */\n  posExpr?: string; velExpr?: string; expr?: string;\n  /** 原版 ai[N]==V 门（不满足跳过该条） */\n  ai?: Array<{ slot: number; value: number; not?: boolean }>;\n  gates?: string[];\n  /** Gore.NewGorePerfect：精确 pos/vel（随机扰动后回写，:1293-1299） */\n  perfect?: boolean;\n  line?: number;\n}\n\n/** 死亡来源 NPC 的最小形状（Enemy/Critter 均满足；测试可传 mock） */\nexport interface GoreNpcSource {\n  vanillaId: number | null;\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;\n  vx: number; vy: number;\n  vanillaScale: number;\n  ai0: number; ai1: number; ai2: number; ai3: number;\n  hitDir?: number;\n}\n\ntype GoreTable = Record<string, GoreSpawnEntry[]> & {\n  _meta: { tex: Record<string, { w: number; h: number }>; [k: string]: unknown };\n};\nconst TABLE = goreTable as unknown as GoreTable;\nconst TEX = TABLE._meta?.tex ?? {};\n\n/** NPC id → 死亡 gore 表项（无表项 = 原版只出尘不出块，如史莱姆族） */\nexport function deathGoreFor(npcId: number): GoreSpawnEntry[] {\n  return TABLE[String(npcId)] ?? [];\n}\n\nconst irand = (n: number) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);\n/** Main.rand.Next(min, max) 语义：[min, max-1] */\nconst irand2 = (min: number, max: number) => min + Math.floor(Math.random() * (max - min));\nconst uniform = (m: number, a: number) => m + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * a;\n\n/** 当前存活 gore 数（NewGore 池满即弃） */\nlet liveGore = 0;\n\nexport class GorePiece extends Entity {\n  /** Gore.type（贴图 Gore_<type>.png） */\n  readonly goreId: number;\n  rotation = 0;\n  scale: number;\n  alpha = 0;\n  timeLeft = GORE_TIME;\n  sticky = true;\n  /** 贴图原始尺寸（碰撞盒与绘制基准；缺失回退 32） */\n  texW: number;\n  texH: number;\n  /** SpriteFrame 行（多帧竖条：1217/1218 等） */\n  row = 0;\n  private frameCounter = 0;\n\n  constructor(type: number, x: number, y: number, scale: number) {\n    super();\n    this.goreId = type;\n    this.scale = scale;\n    const tex = TEX[type];\n    this.texW = tex?.w ?? FALLBACK_TEX;\n    this.texH = tex?.h ?? FALLBACK_TEX;\n    this.x = x;\n    this.y = y;\n    this.resizeBox();\n  }\n\n  /** 碰撞盒 = min(texW,texH)*0.9*scale（Gore.cs:1000-1014） */\n  private resizeBox() {\n    const raw = Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale;\n    this.w = Math.max(2, Math.floor(raw));\n    this.h = this.w;\n  }\n\n  /** Gore.NewGore（Gore.cs:1306-1482）：池满/type 非法则弃；返回 null */\n  static newGore(\n    type: number, x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, scale = 1, perfect = false,\n  ): GorePiece | null {\n    if (type < 0 || liveGore >= MAX_GORE) return null;\n    const g = new GorePiece(type, x, y, scale);\n    g.vx = vx; g.vy = vy;\n    if (!perfect) {\n      // :1347-1348 随机扰动：vy -= Next(10,31)*0.1 ∈ [-3.0,-1.0]；vx += Next(-20,21)*0.1 ∈ [-2.0,2.0]\n      g.vy -= irand2(10, 31) * 0.1;\n      g.vx += irand2(-20, 21) * 0.1;\n    }\n    g.rotation = 0;\n    g.alpha = 0;\n    // :1361-1374 粘滞与时档\n    if (NON_STICKY_TYPES.has(type)) {\n      g.sticky = false;\n    } else if (type >= 375 && type <= 377) {\n      g.sticky = false;\n      g.alpha = 100;\n    } else {\n      g.sticky = true;\n      g.timeLeft = GORE_TIME;\n    }\n    // :1391-1398 多帧族\n    const rows = FRAME_ROWS[type];\n    if (rows) g.row = 0;\n    if (type === 1217 || type === 1218) g.row = 0; // 首帧行由 Update :346-349 随机\n    // :1442-1449（907-909 随机起始帧；本表未出现，保留语义）\n    if (type >= 907 && type <= 909) g.row = irand(3);\n    liveGore++;\n    return g;\n  }\n\n  /** 每固定 tick（Gore.Update，Gore.cs:313-1102） */\n  fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    void dt;\n    if (this.dead) return;\n    const st = game.world.store;\n    const t = this.goreId;\n\n    // sticky 入口：越界消亡 + 速度钳 32（:319-329 / DeactivateIfOutsideOfWorld :297-311）\n    if (this.sticky) {\n      const tx = Math.floor(this.x / 16), ty = Math.floor(this.y / 16);\n      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) { this.kill(); return; }\n      const sp = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);\n      if (sp > 32) { this.vx *= 32 / sp; this.vy *= 32 / sp; }\n    }\n\n    // SpecialAI 7（1218）：UpdateLightningBunnySparks :244-262（switch :331-345 早退）\n    if (t === 1218) {\n      if (this.frameCounter === 0) {\n        this.frameCounter = 1;\n        this.row = irand(3); // Frame(1,3) 内随机行\n      }\n      this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      if (this.timeLeft <= 0) { this.kill(); return; }\n      this.alpha = Math.round(255 - (255 * Math.max(0, this.timeLeft)) / 15);\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (SAIL_TYPES.has(t)) { this.updateSail(game); return; }\n\n    // ---- 位移前物理（:352-769 链）----\n    if (isDampRibbon(t)) {\n      // :352-371 彩带阻尼\n      this.vx *= 0.98;\n      this.vy *= 0.98;\n      if (this.vy < this.scale) this.vy += 0.05;\n      if (this.vy > 0.1) this.rotation += this.vx > 0 ? 0.01 : -0.01;\n      this.vy += 0.2;                 // 默认重力支（:760-764）\n      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;\n    } else if (SHRINK_TYPES.has(t)) {\n      // :535-556 血块收缩（16/17 为 0.01 档——本表未出现，语义保留）\n      this.vy *= 0.98;\n      this.vx *= 0.98;\n      this.scale -= (t === 16 || t === 17) ? 0.01 : 0.007;\n      if (this.scale < 0.1) { this.scale = 0.1; this.alpha = 255; }\n      this.resizeBox();\n    } else {\n      // 默认支（:760-764）：重力 0.2 + 随 vx 翻滚\n      this.vy += 0.2;\n      this.rotation += this.vx * 0.05;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 碰撞/时档尾段（:929-1042）----\n    if (this.sticky) {\n      moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy);\n      if (this.vy === 0) {\n        // 落地摩擦（:1015-1029）：|vx|<0.01 归零\n        this.vx *= 0.97;\n        if (this.vx > -0.01 && this.vx < 0.01) this.vx = 0;\n      }\n      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n    } else {\n      // :1041 非粘滞：两倍速渐隐\n      this.alpha += 2 * vanishAlpha(t);\n      this.x += this.vx;\n      this.y += this.vy;\n    }\n\n    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n  }\n\n  /** Gore_UpdateSail（Gore.cs:1231-1291）：相位下落；碰撞转落地滑移支 */\n  private updateSail(game: GameHooks): void {\n    const t = this.goreId;\n    if (this.vy < 0) {\n      // 落地滑移支（:1233-1261）：只推 velocity.X，不推进 position\n      let moveX = this.vx;\n      const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);\n      const probe = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: moveX, vy: 0.6, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };\n      moveAndCollide(probe, game.world, moveX, 0.6);\n      moveX = probe.vx * 0.97;\n      if (moveX > -0.01 && moveX < 0.01) moveX = 0;\n      if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n      else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n      this.vx = moveX;\n      if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n      return;\n    }\n    // 下落支（:1263-1290）：vy 兼作相位累加器\n    this.vy += Math.PI / 60;\n    let dx = Math.cos(this.vy + Math.PI / 2) * 2 * 2;\n    let dy = Math.abs(Math.sin(this.vy + Math.PI / 2)) * 3 * 2;\n    const size = Math.floor(Math.min(this.texW, this.texH) * 0.9 * this.scale);\n    const body = { x: this.x, y: this.y, w: size, h: size, vx: dx, vy: dy, onGround: false, hitWall: false, hitHead: false };\n    moveAndCollide(body, game.world, dx, dy);\n    if (body.vx !== dx || body.vy !== dy) this.vy = -1; // 碰撞 → 落地支\n    this.x = body.x;\n    this.y = body.y;\n    this.rotation = Math.atan2(body.vy, body.vx) + Math.PI;\n    if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t);\n    else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);\n    if (this.alpha >= 255) this.kill();\n  }\n\n  private kill(): void {\n    if (this.dead) return;\n    this.dead = true;\n    liveGore = Math.max(0, liveGore - 1);\n  }\n\n  /** Renderer 统一绘制（Main.DrawGore Main.cs:21712-21742：中心锚 + scale + rotation） */\n  draw(): void { /* 见 Renderer.drawGorePieces */ }\n\n  /** 多帧行数（1 = 整张贴图） */\n  get frameRows(): number {\n    return FRAME_ROWS[this.goreId] ?? 1;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 具体 gore id（区间/候选均匀取值；不可求值跳过） */\nfunction entryId(e: GoreSpawnEntry): number | null {\n  if (typeof e.id === 'number') return e.id;\n  if (e.idMin != null && e.idMax != null) return e.idMin + irand(e.idMax - e.idMin + 1);\n  if (e.ids?.length) return e.ids[irand(e.ids.length)];\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 出生点（基准 = NPC 左上角；编译字段线性组合，未编译回退原点） */\nfunction entryPos(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { x: number; y: number } {\n  return {\n    x: s.x + (e.ox ?? 0) + (e.fx ?? 0) * s.w + (e.rx ? Math.random() * s.w : 0),\n    y: s.y + (e.oy ?? 0) + (e.fy ?? 0) * s.h + (e.ry ? Math.random() * s.h : 0),\n  };\n}\n\n/** 表项 → 初始速度（缺省继承 NPC 速度；编译字段线性组合） */\nfunction entryVel(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): { vx: number; vy: number } {\n  return {\n    vx: (e.vx ?? (e.kx ?? 1) * s.vx) + (e.ax != null ? uniform(e.mx ?? 0, e.ax) : 0) + (e.hd ? s.hitDir ?? 0 : 0),\n    vy: (e.vy ?? (e.ky ?? 1) * s.vy) + (e.ay != null ? uniform(e.my ?? 0, e.ay) : 0),\n  };\n}\n\n/** 表项 ai 门（原版 `ai[N] == V`）：不满足跳过 */\nfunction aiGateOk(e: GoreSpawnEntry, s: GoreNpcSource): boolean {\n  if (!e.ai?.length) return true;\n  return e.ai.every((g) => {\n    const v = g.slot === 0 ? s.ai0 : g.slot === 1 ? s.ai1 : g.slot === 2 ? s.ai2 : s.ai3;\n    return g.not ? v !== g.value : v === g.value;\n  });\n}\n\n/** 按表炸出某 NPC 的全部死亡 gore；add = 实体入桶回调。返回生成数 */\nexport function spawnDeathGore(s: GoreNpcSource, add: (p: GorePiece) => void): number {\n  if (s.vanillaId == null) return 0;\n  const list = deathGoreFor(s.vanillaId);\n  let n = 0;\n  for (const e of list) {\n    if (!aiGateOk(e, s)) continue;\n    const type = entryId(e);\n    if (type == null || type < 0) continue; // expr 留档条目（提取器不可静态求值）\n    const pos = entryPos(e, s);\n    const vel = entryVel(e, s);\n    const scale = e.scale ?? (e.scaleExpr === 'scale' ? s.vanillaScale : 1);\n    const g = GorePiece.newGore(type, pos.x, pos.y, vel.vx, vel.vy, scale, !!e.perfect);\n    if (g) { add(g); n++; }\n  }\n  return n;\n}\n\n/** Enemy/Critter 死亡钩子：查表炸 gore（props 桶；表空 = 原版只出尘，返回 0） */\nexport function spawnNpcDeathGore(npc: GoreNpcSource, game: GameHooks): number {\n  const add = (game as unknown as {\n    entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, bucket: string) => void };\n  }).entities;\n  if (!add?.add) return 0;\n  return spawnDeathGore(npc, (p) => add.add!(p, 'props'));\n}\n"

    LanternNight.worldClear();      // LanternNight.WorldClear（Main.cs:51656）：运行时态不进存档
    mysticLogs.worldClear();        // MysticLogFairiesEvent.WorldClear（WorldGen.cs:6958）：仙女事件态复位
    cultistWorldClear();            // CultistRitual.recheck 运行时位复位（delay 走存档）
    this.golfState.worldClear();    // GolfState.WorldClear（GolfState.cs:69-76）：轨迹记录/跟踪态不进存档
    this.player = new Player(w.spawnX * TILE + 8, (w.spawnY - 3) * TILE, new Inventory()); // 主角 3 格高，抬高出生点
    // 初始装备 = 原版经典开局三件套（铜镐 3509/铜斧 3506/铜短剑 3507），其余一概不给——
----
    // 天气：雨声层静音 + 环境循环轨停拆 + 渲染器 detach（防下个世界复用旧状态）
    this.stopWeatherLoops();
    this.renderer.weatherFxActive = false;
    this.renderer.weatherFx.detach();
    this.renderer.sky.weatherRef = null;
    this.renderer.sky.clearAmbience();
    natureParticles.clear(); // 落叶/滴水粒子不跨世界残留
    natureParticles.onDripSplash = null;
    // 月总震屏滤镜解除（倒计时中退世界 → 菜单不留红色脉冲）
    this.renderer.moonLordShake = null;
    // 资源释放链(缺一不可,见各 dispose 注释):连续多次读档曾因不释放
    // 累积数百 MB 显存 → contextlost/contextrestored 风暴卡死(2026-08-10 trace)

Both are world-clear paths. I'll use a module-level Set pool in GorePiece.ts (also serves the renderer) plus a `resetGorePool()` export, and add ONE line at Game.ts:1932's block (`this.entities.clear();` followed by other worldClear calls — a natural anchor). Since two sites exist (1932 = new world, ~2251 = exit to menu?), adding to the 1932 site covers world loads; the 2251 site is a dispose path where the renderer detaches. To be thorough without touching two spots: put the reset in the 1932 site (world load) — gore from the old world dies there. The 2251 (exit to menu) leaks the counter but the next world load resets it. Good enough with one line.

Actually — better idea: I can avoid Game.ts entirely! Since pieces live in the `props` bucket and EntityManager.clear() drops references, I can make the pool self-cleaning: GorePiece.fixedUpdate is only called while in a bucket. If I track the pool in a Set AND give each piece an `age`, I still can't detect orphaning...

OK here's the cleanest zero-Game.ts approach: don't keep a module pool at all. Keep the counter, and reset it lazily: when `liveGore >= MAX_GORE` at spawn time, re-count is impossible... but I CAN make the counter conservative: since EntityManager.clear() drops pieces, the count is only wrong upward. A cap that's stuck at max = gore stops spawning after N world loads. Bad.

Decision: add the one line in Game.ts at the world-clear block. It's additive, adjacent to stable anchors (other worldClear calls), and semantically required. Let me now restructure GorePiece to use a Set pool + reset + renderer access.

Design update:
- `const livePool = new Set<GorePiece>()`
- newGore: `if (livePool.size >= MAX_GORE) return null;` then add.
- kill(): delete from set.
- `export function resetGorePool()` → livePool.clear().
- `export function liveGorePieces(): GorePiece[]` → [...livePool] (renderer iterates).

The renderer then calls drawGorePieces(ctx, cam) iterating livePool — no entity filtering needed, and drawing is decoupled from the entity sort (gore isn't y-sorted with NPCs in vanilla either — DrawGore is its own pass).

Let me update GorePiece.ts accordingly.

 "old_string": "    ctx.drawImage(pc, -8, -11, 16, 22);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }",
 "new_string": "    ctx.drawImage(pc, -8, -11, 16, 22);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }\n\n  /** Gore 死亡碎块（Main.DrawGore Main.cs:21712-21742）。世界变换内世界坐标绘制，\n   *  光照合成在后自动受光（同 wind-sway/natureParticles 层）。贴图多帧行取\n   *  GorePiece.frameRows（1217/1218 等竖条）；落地不归零 rotation（Gore.cs 无此步，\n   *  翻滚随 vx 摩擦冻结）。 */\n  private drawGorePieces(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cam: Camera, viewW: number, viewH: number) {\n    if (!this.atlas) return;\n    const pieces = liveGorePieces();\n    if (!pieces.length) return;\n    const z = cam.zoom;\n    // 视区外剔除（扩半贴图防边缘闪断）\n    const x0 = cam.x - viewW / 2 - 64, x1 = cam.x + viewW / 2 + 64;\n    const y0 = cam.y - viewH / 2 - 64, y1 = cam.y + viewH / 2 + 64;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;\n    for (const p of pieces) {\n      if (p.x < x0 || p.x > x1 || p.y < y0 || p.y > y1) continue;\n      this.drawGorePiece(ctx, p, z);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n  }\n\n  private drawGorePiece(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: GorePiece, z: number) {\n    const img = this.atlas!.ensureVImage(`vanilla/Gore_${p.goreId}.png`);\n    if (!img || !img.complete || img.naturalWidth === 0) return;\n    const rows = p.frameRows;\n    const fh = img.naturalHeight / rows;\n    const sh = Math.floor(fh);\n    const sw = img.naturalWidth;\n    // Gore.GetAlpha（:1486）：opacity = (255 - alpha)/255\n    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, (255 - p.alpha) / 255);\n    if (p.rotation === 0 && p.scale === 1) {\n      // 直绘路径（Main.cs:21706-21742 原点即贴图中心：落点 + 尺寸/2）\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, p.row * sh, sw, sh, p.x, p.y, sw, sh);\n      return;\n    }\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(p.x + (sw * p.scale) / 2, p.y + (sh * p.scale) / 2);\n    if (p.rotation) ctx.rotate(p.rotation);\n    if (p.scale !== 1) ctx.scale(p.scale, p.scale);\n    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, p.row * sh, sw, sh, -sw / 2, -sh / 2, sw, sh);\n    ctx.restore();\n    void z;\n  }"
}

Wait, the direct-draw path: vanilla non-frame path draws with origin at texture center: draw position = position + texSize/2 (so the image's top-left lands at `position`), origin texSize/2. Net effect: image drawn at `position` (top-left) with no rotation. So for rotation===0, drawing at (p.x, p.y) top-left is equivalent ✓. But for scale !== 1 the image should be scaled about... vanilla: draw(pos + size/2, origin size/2, scale) → top-left lands at pos (for rot=0) with size*scale. So the scaled image's top-left = p.x, p.y too. My fast path only handles scale===1; otherwise the save/translate path: translate(p.x + sw*scale/2, p.y + sh*scale/2) then scale then draw(-sw/2,-sh/2) → top-left = p.x ✓.

Hmm, but subtle: for scale≠1 the vanilla anchor is the texture CENTER at pos + (texW*scale)/2 ✓ matches mine.

Place piece at y=336-14 (h=14? box = min(texW,texH)*0.9*scale; gore 4 tex = from _meta.tex). Let me not hardcode: construct then use p.h. Place y = 336 - p.h - 0 (just touching? better a few px above with vy=2).

One concern: in the sticky branch I call moveAndCollide(this, game.world, this.vx, this.vy) — Entity has dropThrough/liqFactor etc. defaults fine.

Also, the fade: timeLeft decrements only when? In my code: sticky branch `if (this.timeLeft > 0) this.timeLeft -= vanishSpeed(t); else this.alpha += vanishAlpha(t);` — regardless of landing ✓ (vanilla same).

Test b assertions: after landing tick: vy===0, vx = 2*0.97 (approximately — moveAndCollide may zero vx if wall; no wall here). Then simulate 600 ticks → timeLeft 0; then alpha grows; at 255 → dead=true and removed from pool. Let me compute: timeLeft starts 600; each tick -1 → after 600 ticks timeLeft=0; then alpha += 1 → 255 more ticks → dead at tick ~856. Loop max 1000.

For (c): non-sticky shrink gore 11. Constructor directly (not newGore) so sticky=true default — need sticky=false: use newGore (which sets sticky=false for 11) or set manually. Use newGore(11, ...) then check p.sticky === false, p.alpha === 0. Then fixedUpdate k times.

Let me also verify `uniform` usage: entryVel for EoC entry {mx:0,ax:6} → vx = 0*s.vx? No: e.vx undefined, e.kx undefined → kx default 1 → s.vx + uniform(0,6). Hmm — vanilla: `new Vector2(Next(-30,31)*0.2, ...)` REPLACES velocity entirely, not adds npc velocity! My entryVel adds s.vx (kx default 1) + random. That's WRONG for entries that specify a constant velocity: vanilla NewGore(Position, Velocity) uses the GIVEN velocity as the base (npc velocity NOT added). The random jitter in NewGore adds on top, but the HitEffect-provided velocity replaces.

So the semantics: velocity = entry velocity if the entry specifies one (vx/vy/mx/ax/hd...), else npc velocity. Fix entryVel:
```ts
vx: e.vx ?? (e.ax != null ? uniform(e.mx ?? 0, e.ax) : (e.kx != null ? e.kx * s.vx : (e.hd ? (s.hitDir ?? 0) : s.vx)))
```
Let me restructure cleanly:
- hasConst = e.vx != null
- hasRand = e.ax != null
- hasK = e.kx != null
- hasHd = e.hd != null
- if none → s.vx (inherit)
- else base = hasConst ? e.vx : hasRand ? uniform(...) : hasK ? e.kx*s.vx : 0; then if hasHd and not hasConst/hasRand/hasK → base = hitDir. Since hd entries are `new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` (vx=hitDirection, vy=0).

Simplest: compute per-axis:
```ts
function axisVel(v, k, m, a, hd, inherit, hitDir) {
  if (v != null) return v;
  if (a != null) return uniform(m ?? 0, a);
  if (k != null) return k * inherit;
  if (hd) return hitDir ?? 0;
  return inherit;
}
```
That's exact. Let me rewrite entryVel.

Also check: entries with velExpr (uncompiled locals like `vector9`) → all fields undefined → inherit npc velocity ✓ intended fallback.

Let me fix entryVel now.

```
#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const rest = [];
  for (const a of argv) {
    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }

```

/** 按表炸出某 NPC 的全部死亡 gore；add = 实体入桶回调。返回生成数 */
export function spawnDeathGore(s: GoreNpcSource, add: (p: GorePiece) => void): number {
  if (s.vanillaId == null) return 0;
  const list = deathGoreFor(s.vanillaId);
  let n = 0;
  for (const e of list) {
    if (!aiGateOk(e, s)) continue;
    const times = e.loop ?? 1;
    for (let k = 0; k < times; k++) {
      const type = entryId(e);
      if (type == null || type < 0) continue; // expr 留档条目（提取器不可静态求值）
      const pos = entryPos(e, s);
      const vel = entryVel(e, s);
      const scale = e.scale ?? (e.scaleExpr === 'scale' ? s.vanillaScale : 1);
      const g = GorePiece.newGore(type, pos.x, pos.y, vel.vx, vel.vy, scale, !!e.perfect);
      if (g) { add(g); n++; }
    }
  }
  return n;
}

/** Enemy/Critter 死亡钩子：查表炸 gore（props 桶；表空 = 原版只出尘，返回 0）。
 *  hitDir = HitEffect 的 hitDirection（击退方向，`new Vector2(hitDirection, 0f)` 类表项用） */

    entities?: { add?: (p: unknown, bucket: string) => void };
  }).entities;
